# 01 — roop-ultimate complete audit (reference repo, read-only)

Repo: `rishabh4496/roop-ultimate`, commit `71ebb65c5fa5f11a72e343e8fc4f285bf591b555`, shallow-cloned from GitHub into an OS temp directory (outside the `roop-3d` checkout; never written, never imported). Tracked-file inventory: **2118 files / 33.3 MB** (`_inventory/roop_ultimate_files.tsv`, from the GitHub git-tree API; `truncated: false`).
Method: four parallel read-only auditors owned disjoint prefixes; every file has a row (per-file for source/config, per-group-with-file-names for the 717 `app/tests` files). Very large modules (`ProcessMgr.py`, `api.py`, `procmgr_tracking.py`) were outlined and read by range, not line-by-line. Unverified statements are tagged `[INFERENCE]`.
Slices: **RU_A** `app/roop`, `app/core|clip|ui|sidecar_keep|tools|docs`, `app/*` top-level (387) · **RU_B** `face_engine`, `tools`, `tests`, `web_ui`, `benchmarks`, `scripts`, `portable`, root (309) · **RU_C** `react-ui`, `docs`, `app/tests` (1422). 387+309+1422 = 2118.
Caveat: roop-ultimate's own `docs/audits/STAGE3–STAGE9_*` are flagged synthetic by their authors (STAGE5 "INVALID - DO NOT CITE"); no number from them is used here.

## Executive summary (orchestrator synthesis)

| Axis | roop-ultimate reality | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 Detection / angle | SCRFD/RetinaFace default (thr 0.50, 640 px), 5-pt kps for all swappers; pose from 5-pt weak-perspective solve (`solve_pose_5pt`, "measured 15–20° off per person"); angle bins are coarse (profile = \|yaw\|>45°); landmark symmetry repair refused beyond \|yaw\|>55°; hififace lateral fade from 35°, off at 65°; Stage-0 extreme-profile scenario: **25/30 detection failures, ID 0.467, 4.36 fps** | `app/roop/face_util.py:2818-2970`, `app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:91-136`, `face_engine` benchmark row |
| 2 2D vs 3D | Purely 2D similarity warp (112/128→256 crops). `use_3d_recon` is off by default and a documented no-op for the inswapper family; `fit_source`/`render_from_coefficients` are stubs. `.fsz` = ZIP of PNGs + `metadata.json` v2 (3D-68 landmarks + yaw/pitch/roll per source), no mesh | `FaceSwap.jsx:4007`, `app/roop/faceset_v2.py` |
| 3 Quality | Mask = box × XSeg × BiSeNet; LAB colour transfer; GFPGAN/CodeFormer/GPEN; Ultra Restore frequency split (σ 2.5); swap resolution fixed 128/256 (PixelBoost polyphase to 1024) | `face_engine/masker.py:1-5`, `ultra_engine.py:40-50` |
| 4 Temporal | Kalman + Hungarian tracker (coast 15), ByteTrack exists in `face_engine` but has **no production caller**, histogram cut detectors, LK flow, EMA 0.75; High-motion scenario instability 0.855 | `procmgr_tracking.py`, `tracker.py:512-714` |
| 5 CLI / telemetry | `run.py` CLI (args at `run.py:164-228`), ANSI terminal dashboard mirrored into the UI, `/ws/telemetry`, `tools/audit_system.py` (1124 lines) | RU_A appendix, `TERMINAL_CONTRACT.md` |
| 6 Compute | TRT→CUDA→CPU, FP32 locks on swap nets/XSeg/landmarks/GPEN-1024+, INT8/FP8 rejected on RTX 4070, VRAM governor batch 8→1, RTX 3060 validation pending | `PRECISION_POLICY.md`, `trt_compiler.py:269` |
| 7 Queue / IO | Queue persisted to `queue.json`, sha256-checked resumable segments, GOP segment worker pool, libx264/NVENC, A/V sync check; `face_engine` has a single in-memory job | RU_A §Resume, `face_engine/server/state.py:113-126` |
| 8 UI | `react-ui` (377 files): NLE timeline, diagonal split-wipe compare, head-pose HUD, pose radar, mask brush + roto, VR180 viewer, 38 themes. **No 3D mesh viewport** | RU_C §4 |

Features worth porting or surpassing are enumerated in report 03.

## RU_A — roop-ultimate backend audit (slice A)

Reference repo: roop-ultimate @ 71ebb65 (read-only clone). Scope: `app/roop/` (289), `app/core/`, `app/clip/`, `app/ui/`, `app/sidecar_keep/`, `app/tools/`, `app/docs/` and every top-level `app/*` file (selected with the assignment regex, incl. non-code top-level files such as README/Dockerfile/LICENSE/yaml/`--help`).
Method: AST index of every file (docstring, top-level symbols with line numbers, stub-keyword counts) + 9 parallel per-group readers who read headers, constants, route decorators, argparse blocks and fallback paths; very large files (ProcessMgr.py 450 KB, procmgr_tracking.py 230 KB, api.py 379 KB, face_util.py 181 KB, runtime_optimizer.py 163 KB, FaceSwapInsightFace.py 136 KB, core.py 122 KB, optimized_processor.py 119 KB, procmgr_masking.py 118 KB, settings.py 115 KB) were outlined and read by targeted ranges, **not line-by-line**. Evidence paths are normalised to repo-relative (`app/...:line`); a few bare short names in prose are relative to `app/roop/` (stated per group in the origin tag). Unverified claims are tagged `[INFERENCE]`.

### Executive summary (what this reference actually is)
- **Pure 2D swap pipeline.** SCRFD/RetinaFace detect -> 5-pt (or 106-pt) similarity alignment -> inswapper-class 128 px tile (pixel-boost tiles up to `subsample_size`) -> mask/feather/colour transfer -> optional restorer -> paste. No volumetric/.fsz 3D code exists; "3D" = `use_3d_recon` (PnP + 2D flip/shear, `fit_source`/`render_from_coefficients` are no-op stubs), frontalization, pose-binned source bank; `.fsz` is a ZIP of `N.png` + `metadata.json` v2 (RUA_GrpA, frag A).
- **Strength is engineering around the 2D path**: Kalman+Hungarian identity tracks with stitching/coasting, One-Euro and zero-phase smoothing, histogram scene-cut detectors, TensorRT/ORT ladder with precision policy + VRAM governor, segmented resumable video writer with sha256 manifests, persistent queue, WebSocket telemetry, terminal dashboard, React workstation API (66 inline routes + 29 routers).
- **Many advanced modules are opt-in, unwired, or contract-only** (see Gaps): `fused_postprocess`, `l2_persistence`, `vectorized_pipeline`, `inference_optimizer`, `unified_runtime_scheduler`, `trt_engine`, `worker_pool`, `video_io_hw` zero-copy classes, `JournalManager`, `final_quality_gate`, `stage17_audit`; `UltraMax compile` route is simulated; `tile_diffusion` is not diffusion.
- Numerous features were *measured worse* and are shipped OFF (bystander veto, flow-align, hi-res redetect, nonfrontal mask routing, mask fusion, occluder neutral on synthetic occluders).

### api.py infrastructure [E1a]
- CORS: `allow_origin_regex=^https?://(localhost|127.0.0.1|[::1]|*.localhost)(:port)?$`, credentials True, methods/headers `*`, exposes Content-Range/Accept-Ranges/Content-Length/Content-Type (app/api.py:111-120).
- Auth: outermost `AccessControlMiddleware` (app/api.py:122): foreign Origin -> 403 "Origin not allowed"; share mode needs token (Bearer / `?token=` / cookie `roop_api_token`) else 401; WS refused with close 1008; OPTIONS bypasses token (app/api_access.py:190-215). Non-share = no token at all (app/api_access.py:108-109). Origin check passes when no Origin header (app/api_access.py:81-82).
- Upload errors -> 400 `{detail}` (app/api.py:125-128). Upload roots registered: temp/api_uploads, temp, .pinokio-temp (app/api.py:304-312).
- WebSocket: no @app.websocket in api.py; `/ws/telemetry` lives in app/routes_telemetry.py:289 (outside group). Lifespan (app/api.py:7243-7264) binds loop + starts `hub.start_sampler(_telemetry_snapshot)` (side-effect-free snapshot, app/api.py:7091-7240), assigned via `app.router.lifespan_context` (not constructor/on_event). No SSE anywhere; StreamingResponse only used for in-memory multipart-ish preview_grid/preview_seq (app/api.py:2995,3178).
- Static UI: serves react-ui/dist; `/assets` mount + 404 handler SPA fallback; 503 HTML "React UI Build Missing" if dist/index.html absent (app/api.py:7270-7512).
- Concurrency guards: `_preview_request_lock` (app/api.py:131); 409 on preview while render active (app/api.py:197-203); 503 busy retry_after_ms=1000 during pre-pass stage (app/api.py:206-222); 503 "initializing" retry 500ms until CFG published (app/api.py:225-232); stale-selection guard via `_selection_versions` (app/api.py:140-146); /api/swap 409 if processing or benchmark running (app/api.py:5611-5622), 400 if no target/source.
- Angle-bank gates (env-overridable, scipy cosine distance): ROOP_ANGLE_MANUAL_MAX 0.90, ACCEPT 0.60, SEED_MAX 0.85, MIN_QUALITY 0.35, MIN_PX 64, LONE_ACCEPT 0.55, REVIEW 0.70 (app/api.py:321-373). BLUR_FRAC/WARMUP owned by face_quality.
- Pose bins (axis 1): coverage-only pose from 5 kps, yaw=log(|nose-lex|/|rex-nose|), pitch=log(|ny-eye_y|/|mouth_y-ny|); YAW_EDGES (-1.20,-0.75,-0.43,-0.15,0.15,0.43,0.75,1.20) (9 bins), PITCH_EDGES (-0.75,-0.43,0.37,0.75) (5 bins) (app/api.py:1993-2042). Heuristic keypoint ratio, not a head-pose solver. UI label "Front" ~±45° yaw (app/api.py:1991).
- Pre-pass/tracking: pose-transition re-scan of coarse sweep gaps (app/api.py:2045-2065); pause controller (`_procmgr_runtime.pause_controller`) for /api/pause,/resume (app/api.py:6338-6366).

### Appendix: run.py CLI arguments (run.py:164-228) and env [E1a]
`--cuda_device_id` int 0; `--execution-provider` ; `--execution-batch-size` (batch_size_arg, beats perf_batch_max); `--source/--source-path`; `--resume`; `--project`; `--render`; `--output`; `--export-fcpxml`; `--export-edl`; `--scene-detect` (all except --project need --project else parser.error); `--benchmark`; `--benchmark-faces {1,2,all}` default 1; `--benchmark-mode {quick,full,regression}` default quick (quick ~30s, full ~90s, regression = real 300-frame 1080p render with fps/VRAM/latency/SSIM/PSNR); `--benchmark-frames` 300; `--benchmark-clip`; `--benchmark-source`; `--benchmark-threads`; `--benchmark-update-baseline`; `--benchmark-apply`; `--ui {react,gradio,legacy}`; `--react`; `--diagnose-runtime`. api_access also reads argv `--server_share` (not in argparse: run.py parser would reject unknown arg [INFERENCE: argparse.parse_args errors on --server_share unless defined elsewhere]).
Env set/read: PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF, OPENCV_LOG_LEVEL=ERROR, AV_LOG_LEVEL=error, ROOP_REACT_CLIENT, ROOP_HEADLESS_PROJECT, ROOP_API_PORT (8001), ROOP_ANGLE_* (7 names), ROOP_PREVIEW_PNG, and ENV_SETTINGS exports (ROOP_TRT_POOL, TRT_BUILDER_OPT_LEVEL, TRT_AUX_STREAMS, TRT_CUDA_GRAPH, CV_THREADS, ORT_INTRA/INTER_THREADS, FFMPEG_THREADS, DETMASK_POOL, DETECTOR_POOL, EXPR_POOL, ENCODER_PRESET, STAB_CHUNK_MB, STAB_STREAMING, CUDA_ARENA_STRATEGY, CUDNN_CONV_ALGO, CUDA_MEM_LIMIT, PROFILE, BATCH_SWAP(+_XFRAME), NVDEC, BATCH_SWAP_MAX, NVENC_PRESET, GPU_AFFINE, PINNED_BUFFERS, TEMPORAL_STEP, PREPASS_DETECT_INTERVAL, PREPASS_FILTER, ENHANCE_MIN_FACE_PX, AUTO_BYPASS_RESTORER_NATIVE_SWAP, TEMPORAL_SMART_SKIP, SPECULAR_HIGHLIGHT_RECOVERY, AMBIENT_SHADOW_MATCH, SUBDERMAL_WARMTH_MATCH, HF_FLOW_{BACKEND,VRAM,WEIGHTS,CYCLE_SIGMA,BILATERAL_SIGMA}, FACE_DEMARCATE, TRACK_STITCH, VERIFY_SWAP, UPRIGHT_REMEASURE, ADAFACE, HDR, HDR_TRANSFER, HDR_PRIMARIES, HDR_CODEC, IDENTITY_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, PRIORITY) (app/settings.py:138-210); explicit env wins except mib_to_bytes (app/settings.py:268-271).
Startup flow: DLL dirs registered -> (main only) run_preflight_checks (BOOT, DEPENDENCY, DLL_RUNTIME, ORT, GPU, PROVIDER_ADMISSION phases, app/run.py:86-105) -> UTF-8 stdout -> _apply_perf_env (RuntimeOptimizer.startup_profile + settings.apply_env) -> CPU topology log -> win asyncio compat -> argparse -> diagnose-runtime exit -> batch size -> provider override into globals.execution_providers -> main: --resume | --benchmark(regression/other) | headless project render via core.run() | else keep_awake.boost_process_priority, start run_api daemon thread, core.run(), then (React) API_READY/UI_READY phases + join loop (app/run.py:325-435).

### Appendix: Benchmark metrics [D]
- Standard window 150 frames (5 s @30 fps), master clip 300 frames 1920x1080@30; autotune screen 100f / confirm 600f; acceptance 600f; ranking 120f (app/roop/benchmark/asset_manager.py:41-46; app/roop/benchmark/autotune.py:48-49; app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:98-103). Metrics stored: avg_fps, p1_low_fps, peak_vram_mb, peak_cpu_pct (app/roop/benchmark/storage.py:144). Dual-target required metrics: baseline_fps, final_fps, improvement_pct, peak/avg VRAM, CPU/GPU util, decode/inference/enhancement/encode throughput, latency_ms, stability, output_quality (app/roop/hardware_validation.py:19-26). Bench profiles: quick 1.0 s measure / pools (1,); full 1.5 s x2 reps / pools (1,2,3,4,6) / est 450 s; providers est 120 s; stress 12 s x2 / default faces 2.0 / est 900 s (app/roop/bench.py:79-155). Thread/pool gain bars THREAD_GAIN 5%, POOL_GAIN 4%, stability spread 20% (app/roop/bench.py:168-181). Regression thresholds: face SSIM mean >=.97, per-frame min >=.90, face PSNR >=35, frame PSNR >=40, coverage drop <=.02, fps warn 15% advisory (app/roop/benchmark/regression.py:75-92).

### Axis findings (slice)
Bullets are tagged `[group]` (see Group map). Evidence is `file:line`.

#### (1) Detection / landmarks / angle estimation + failure thresholds
- [F] Config detection defaults: `detector_engine: retinaface_r50`, `face_detector_threshold 0.5`, `face_detector_nms 0.3`, `face_detector_size '512'`, `max_face_distance 0.75`, `refine_landmarks true`, `rescue_small_faces true`, `autorotate_faces true`, `detector_scale_pyramid auto`, `upright_remeasure auto` (`app/default_config.yaml:20,31-34,57,101-102,158`). Schema defaults differ: `face_detector_size 640` (`settings.schema.json`, key `face_detector_size`).
- [F] Pose gates are in backend constants only named in `--help` (face_util: `FACE_ROLL_LOWER/UPPER`, `POSE_ALIGN_ONSET_DEG`, `POSE_ALIGN_FULL_DEG`, `YAW_ALIGN_RATIO`; no values dumped) — [INFERENCE] values not recoverable from this group.
- [F] Occlusion landmark-symmetry repair: refused past `|yaw| > 55°` because `solve_pose_5pt` is "measured 15–20° off per person"; mirror map accepted only as an involution `m[m[i]]==i`, scale-free tolerance; works for any landmark count (`docs/PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` §2).
- [F] UI face selection modes: "First found", "All input faces", "All female", "All male", "All faces", "Selected face" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:30`). No-face actions: "Use untouched original frame", "Retry rotated", "Skip Frame", "Skip Frame if no similar face", "Use last swapped" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:29`); default `Use untouched original frame` (`app/default_config.yaml:71`).
- [F] UI "Max Face Similarity Threshold" slider 0.01–1.0, "0.0 = identical 1.0 = no similarity" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:251`); Face Mgr cut uses `extract_face_images(filename,(True,frame_num),0.5)` threshold 0.5 (`facemgr_tab.py:~147`).
- [F] Recognition calibration (equal-error cosine-distance thresholds from 16-clip set): default w600k 0.700, adaface 0.698, glintr100 0.704, antelopev2 0.704, mobilefacenet 0.631, facerecognizersf 0.557 (`app/tools/compare_recognition_swap.py:52-53`). Min face 48 px; dup IoU 0.35; link IoU 0.5; cut diff 25.0 on 64x36 thumbs (`app/tools/calibrate_recognition.py:42-46`). Only w600k and AdaFace are wired into the real pipeline's matching; other four are offline emulation (`compare_recognition_swap.py` docstring).
- [F] Selection-invariance (audit): CPU vs CUDA vs TRT per-frame swap decision identical 119/119 for both persons on `double/d1.mp4`; selection sensitivity (two faces) NOT TESTED; `double/d2,d6` selected-single-person yields 0/200 composited — "known open lead single-person fallback on multi-face content" (`RUNTIME_PROVIDER_AUDIT.md` §4).
- [E1a] Detection defaults in settings: detector_engine `retinaface_r50`, face_detector_size 640 (320 if default_det_size False), threshold 0.50, nms 0.3, detector_scale_pyramid auto, rescue_small_faces True, temporal_detection True, autorotate_faces True, max_face_distance 0.75, face_detection_mode "Selected face" (app/settings.py:1098-1106,1352-1356). Pose = keypoint-ratio bins only (app/api.py:1993-2042); no pitch/yaw failure threshold in group beyond identity_lock_margin 0.08/hold 3 (app/settings.py:1349-1350) and frontalization_threshold 15.0 deg (app/settings.py:1162, off by default `use_frontalization` False).
- [D] Detector static input pinned 640x640 for scrfd/retinaface family under TRT (app/roop/trt_shape_profile.py:113-124); detector_resolution autotune 512/640/768/960 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1590-1605). Detect pre-pass (optimized_prepass.PrepassConfig): interval 5, dim 640, min score 0.0, max faces 32, 5-pt fallback from fixed box ratios (0.32/0.38, .68/.38, .5/.55, .37/.72, .63/.72) (app/roop/optimized_prepass.py:98-160,206-226). RetinaFaceDecoder conf .5 NMS .4 top_k 64 (app/roop/vectorized_pipeline.py:535-545; unwired).
- [D] No yaw/pitch estimation code lives in group D. Benchmark scenarios assume yaw 30-50 deg (45deg_face), 70-80 deg (extreme profile) and a ~56x56 px small-face (app/roop/benchmark/scenario_assets.py:83-203); `quality_metrics` profile failure = zero detections on `is_profile` frames (app/roop/benchmark/quality_metrics.py:164-168) and uses supplied ground-truth yaw, not an estimator.
- [D] Identity-mismatch threshold in bench quality: cosine < 0.20 counted as incorrect assignment (app/roop/benchmark/quality_metrics.py:208); regression changed-face MAD 4.0/255 (5.6x the measured 0.714 noise floor) (app/roop/benchmark/regression.py:70-73).
- [B] All swap models use 5-pt kps alignment; no 3D mesh. Template per model: arcface (inswapper/reswapper/hyperswap/alpha), arcface_112_v1 (ghost, simswap), ffhq_512 (blendswap, uniface, cscs, instyleswapper A/B/C), mtcnn_512 (hififace) — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:113-533.
- [B] Yaw from 5-pt: `face_util.solve_pose_jaw_5pt` (outside group) used by RealSwap: hififace eye-band fades for |yaw|>=35 deg (ROOP_REALSWAP_LATERAL_FADE_DEG) and is fully off at 65 deg (_LATERAL_SKIP_DEG) — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:1773-1774,2077-2091.
- [B] Outcome guard (swap_moved_the_face): per-model verify_tol; only hififace sets 0.65 (default global SWAP_MOVED_TOL=1.0 in face_util); calibrated on ONE synthetic clip; hyperswap/inswapper clean-vs-wrecked bands overlap (0.79 vs 0.79 / 0.60) so not tightened — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:206-233,407,567.
- [B] Legacy frame swapper: roll normalisation when |theta|>45 deg (build_canonical_rotation_matrix threshold_deg=45.0); profile-aware weighted Umeyama when |yaw|>35 — app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1881-1990 (comments at ~1900-1975). Fallback stubs of these helpers return identity/None (app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:263-292).
- [B] Adaptive enhancer pose score = 1 - max(|yaw|,|pitch|)/65; gates: pose<0.18, conf<0.28, occlusion>0.72, temporal<0.28 => no enhancer; quality cut FAST 0.63 / BALANCED 0.68 / REALISTIC 0.72 / MAX 0.76 (hysteresis +-0.06); 5-pt fallback yaw = |nose-eyemid|/IOD*45 — app/roop/adaptive_enhancer.py:87-104,172-200.
- [B] Restore Ultra optimizer damps for |yaw|>25: eye factor max(0.55,1-(|yaw|-25)/70), edge factor max(0.70,1-(|yaw|-25)/90); crispness multiplier clamp [0.35,1.10] — app/roop/restore_ultra_optimizer.py:291-304.
- [B] MAR (mouth open): 68-pt = (|p61-p67|+|p62-p66|+|p63-p65|)/(2|p60-p64|), threshold default 0.18 (clamped 0.05-0.50); 106-pt path is a different estimator (r=0.49 vs 68-pt); 5-pt returns constant 0.10 — app/roop/enhancers/mouth_dynamics.py:57-131,137-141.
- [B] EAR blink threshold: code const EAR_BLINK_THRESHOLD=0.18, module docstring says 0.21 (contradiction) — app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:16,320.
- [B] XSeg3 mask-cache recompute triggers: |d yaw/pitch/roll|>2.5 deg, landmark displacement >1.8 px, d confidence < -0.15 — app/roop/xseg3_optimizer.py:16-24 (docstring).
- [B] Enhance gate: skip restorer when face px < ROOP_ENHANCE_MIN_FACE_PX (default 0 = disabled), resume at 1.15x threshold — app/roop/enhance_gate.py:33,38-41,90.
- [E2] Not in this group. Only related: live tracker keeps face points from `landmark_2d_106`→`landmark_3d_68`→`kps` (first with ≥3 finite 2D points), live_mode.py:~232-243. No yaw/pose limits anywhere in E2 files.
- [E2] Live LK-flow reject gates: <max(3, 55%) surviving points → keyframe re-detect; median LK error >35.0 → reject; RANSAC `estimateAffinePartial2D` reproj 3.0 px; winSize (15,15), maxLevel 2, 10 iters/eps 0.03, live_mode.py:~268-286.
- [E2] Watchdog: all-zero/NaN/Inf tensor = anomaly; mask area drop to 0 or >70% jump (prev ≥100 px) within same scene cut and consecutive frame, app/roop/watchdog.py:380-431; zero-swap alert after 5 consecutive frames, app/roop/watchdog.py:346-357.
- [A] Detector engines (roop.globals.detector_engine, default 'scrfd' globals.py:292): scrfd (insightface buffalo_l det_10g), yoloface, retinaface (10g), retinaface_r50, retinaface_r50_gpu, yunet (`_HYBRID_ENGINES` app/roop/face_util.py:79). Hybrid engines pop buffalo_l's own detection session and reuse its aux models (app/roop/face_util.py:291-293).
- [A] Defaults: face_detector_size '640' (globals.py:58), face_detector_threshold 0.50 (globals.py:59; _build_face_analyser fallback 0.60 at app/roop/face_util.py:267), face_detector_nms 0.40 (globals.py:60). `_desired_det_size` clamps 320..1280 (app/roop/face_util.py:70). scrfd/retinaface/retinaface_r50 are FORCED to 640 regardless of setting (app/roop/face_util.py:1076-1077,1089-1090). yoloface export is fixed 640 (app/roop/yoloface.py:62-96); yunet scales longest side to det_size, score .6 / nms .3 at creation, then per-call thresh (app/roop/yunet.py:29,100-117); R50 priors min_sizes ((16,32),(64,128),(256,512)), steps (8,16,32), variance (.1,.2) (app/roop/retinaface.py:47-49).
- [A] Landmark counts: 5-pt kps (all engines), landmark_2d_106 (always in allowed modules, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:1333), landmark_3d_68 (insightface 3D-68 coords, loaded when use_3d_recon/use_source_bank/use_frontalization/refine_landmarks/autorotate, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:1329-1335; 'lazy' for autorotate-only, app/roop/face_util.py:289-290). No 203/478-pt landmarks anywhere in group A (only mentions: none found in scanned symbols).
- [A] Pose: primary estimator is `solve_pose_5pt` weak-perspective pinv solve against `_REF3D_68`-derived 5-pt head (app/roop/face_util.py:2818-2970); '<0.05 deg on synthetic grid yaw 0-90, pitch +/-40, roll +/-30' (docstring 2948-2950). `solve_pose_jaw_5pt` adds a 6th DOF for mouth opening, JAW_SOLVE_MIN/MAX -0.35/0.90 (app/roop/face_util.py:2919-2920). `pose_quality.estimate_head_pose` = perspective EPnP + solvePnPRefineLM against same ref head, rotated to line of sight (app/roop/pose_quality.py:131-168). face_analyser.estimate_head_pose_pnp uses a different canonical 3D-5 template (app/roop/face_analyser.py:63-69,138). Sign convention: +yaw = face toward viewer's right, +pitch = tilted DOWN (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:9-22).
- [A] Angle bins (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:91-136): 9-bin lattice on up-positive pitch: FRONTAL |yaw|<=10 & |p|<=10; QUARTER L/R yaw 10-25; HALF_PROFILE L/R yaw 25-45 (|p|<=15); PROFILE L/R |yaw|>45 (any pitch); PITCH_UP/DOWN |yaw|<=20 & |p|>15. Gaps exist; MAX_FILL_DEG=10 nearest-fill (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:54). Per-person neutral pitch (median of |yaw|<=25) subtracted (140-160).
- [A] FaceSet V2 yaw ladder POSE_BINS (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:37-40,226-240): frontal <=10, mild <=25, medium <=45, strong <=70, profile >70. 3x3 pose matrix edges yaw +/-25, pitch +/-15 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:89-93). Pose-Radar buckets (app/roop/identity_lab.py:21-31): profile |yaw|>=40, angle |pitch|>=20.
- [A] Profile thresholds elsewhere: alignment switches at |yaw|>=45 or |pitch|>=30 (app/roop/face_analyser.py:660-667); source_portfolio PROFILE_YAW_DEG 35 (app/roop/source_portfolio.py:46); adaptive_detector profile_yaw_threshold 45 (app/roop/adaptive_detector.py:51); geometric_alignment semi 20/full 45 (app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:42-43); nonfrontal off-axis max 50 deg, yaw-ratio min .55, asym max .25, pitch band .32-.70 (app/roop/nonfrontal.py:49-54); source_gallery label heuristic ratio <.65 / >1.55 (app/source_gallery.py:63-66); face_3d_recon flip needs |yaw|>=20 both sides, opposite sign, |delta|>=45 (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:190-191).
- [A] Roll/upside-down: FACE_ROLL_LOWER 54.5, FACE_ROLL_UPPER 135 (app/roop/face_util.py:2498-2499); orientation.py uses 45/135 with hysteresis 36/54 and 126/144 (app/roop/orientation.py:45-46,171-210); track latch TRUST_DEG 40, MAX_COAST 45 (app/roop/orientation.py:53,58); lm68 arm at 20 deg, probe every 12, hold 90, disagree 45 (app/roop/face_util.py:1427-1433). Detector hallucinates upright face at roll 140-210 (app/roop/face_util.py:2515-2519).
- [A] Failure/rescue ladder when first pass empty (app/roop/face_util.py:1918-1957): upscale x2 (only if rescue_small_faces, default False globals.py:287) -> downscaled 320 with thresh max(0.30, thr-0.15) -> padded 25% -> rotated -> CLAHE; plus partial-miss rotated passes for expected_count (1969-1992). A second-detector rescue was measured and REJECTED (app/roop/face_util.py:1959-1965).
- [A] Pyramid: auto-trigger when face h>=500px or >=75% of frame, scales [0.5,0.75,1.0], 64px context pad, DIoU-NMS (app/roop/face_detector.py:53-57,394-437,676-681); explicit via --detector-scale-pyramid.
- [A] Dedup/touching faces: NMS suppresses only if IoU>thr AND centre sep <=0.25 face-widths (app/roop/nms.py:50,97-102); phantom 'junction' box removal thresholds in app/roop/face_contact.py:92-148; sanity validator drops faces with bad bbox/kps (face_sanity.py header).
- [A] Landmark sanity validators: adaptive_detector interocular>=3px etc. (app/roop/adaptive_detector.py:127-135); capture_seed.landmarks_plausible mouth below eyes by 5% box, nose between (app/roop/capture_seed.py:57-86).
- [A] Quality gates for angle capture: MIN_IOD_PX 45 frontal-equivalent, luma 40..215, clipped <=0.25, EAR>=0.20, blur relative 0.5*median after 8 samples (app/roop/pose_quality.py:79-88; app/roop/face_quality.py:143-165; app/roop/pose_quality.py:290-296).
- [C1] Track appearance gate EMB_MAX default 0.7 (`ROOP_TRACK_EMB_MAX`), Re-ID vs retired track 0.5 (`ROOP_TRACK_REID_MAX`), IOU_MIN 0.2, STALE 15 frames: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:261,305; app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:428.
- [C1] Identity veto 0.85 (`ROOP_TRACK_VETO`), single-person absolute veto OFF (0), margin 0.15, assign max 0.6, assign margin 0.15, floor 0.45 (AdaFace floor 0.45), selected hold 0.95, unbound-same 0.65, min obs 3, overlap frac 0.15: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:219,239,265,458,516,538,555,569,585,481,489.
- [C1] Locked-track detector-confidence floor LOCK_MIN_DET=0.85: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:1037. Verify-swap outcome gate on by default, VERIFY_AWAY_YAW 88.0, MOVED_TOL 0.5, SHAPE_TOL 0.3: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:973-1020.
- [C1] Hi-res miss redetect OFF by default (HIRES_DET_SIZE 960), ROI-crop redetect OFF; both documented as measured regressions: app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:321,363-364. Prepass rescue every 8 frames: tracking:515.
- [C1] Rotation retry: retry_rotated rotates frame 90 CW/CCW and re-runs swap_faces; accepted only if `rotation_improves_upright` unless track-resolved roll: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:3605-3660, 5693-5710. Rotated paste-back uses `_unrotate_face_to_parent` into full-frame space.
- [C1] Partial-miss ROI rescue default ON (`ROOP_NO_PARTIAL_MISS_RESCUE`): app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:231; per-worker last_found_bboxes app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:3913. ROI pad = 0.6*max(w,h) (verify), app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5310.
- [C1] Frontalization (Option 2) only if `use_frontalization`; default threshold 25.0 deg pitch/yaw; via roop.face_frontalize.frontalize_crop: app/roop/ProcessOptions.py:23, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:6193-6215. Pose via estimate_pose on 68-lm crop: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5861.
- [C1] Mask recovery: undersized-mask floor from 5 kps ellipse, trigger 0.5 of floor area, `ROOP_MASK_RECOVER` default on: app/roop/procmgr_masking.py:179-224.
- [C1] Nonfrontal unwarped-box mask routing default '0' (off), 'auto' routes by pose, '1' always: app/roop/procmgr_masking.py:95-117.
- [E1b] globals defaults: face_detector_threshold 0.50 (comment: lowered from 0.60 for slight angles), face_detector_nms 0.40, face_detector_size '640', detector_scale_pyramid 'auto', detector_padding_border 64 px, detector_padding_mode 'reflect', adaptive_detection True, adaptive_detect_interval 8, adaptive_motion_threshold 0.05, adaptive_roi_rescue True, distance_threshold 1, no_face_action 1, track_identities True, sparse_auto_range False, temporal_detection False, identity_lock False (margin 0.08, hold 3) (globals.py:~66-122,150,230-240).
- [E1b] utilities.pre_rotate_face_crop: roll > 30.0 deg (default `threshold=30.0`) triggers upright-rotation around bbox centre with BORDER_REPLICATE, otherwise identity matrices (app/roop/utilities.py:300-320). `profile_alignment_matrix` builds a similarity (cv2.estimateAffinePartial2D LMEDS, scale-ratio guard 1.0001) from 3 profile anchors (visible ear/tragus, nose tip, chin) to a template at (0.50±0.25 s, 0.48), (0.50, 0.52), (0.50, 0.80) of crop size; side chosen by yaw sign (>=0 -> +1); NO yaw limit is enforced here (app/roop/utilities.py:323-362). This is 2D similarity only, never 3D.
- [E1b] core.AdaptiveLODDispatcher: LOD0_THRESHOLD 120.0, LOD1_THRESHOLD 350.0 on face bbox diagonal D (core.py:~1225-1226,1138-1200): D<120 -> LOD0 swap_size 128; 120<=D<=350 -> LOD1 swap_size 256, gpen_size 256; D>350 -> LOD2 swap_size 512, gpen_size 512. Dispatch is by diagonal only (no angle input).
- [E1b] routes_diagnostics.settings_advisor samples frames at 0.25/0.5/0.75 of the clip (:~145) and recommends: coverage<0.9 -> temporal_detection + RetinaFace over SCRFD; min face <5% frame -> rescue_small_faces / det size 640; max face >45% -> subsample_upscale 512px; >22% & 128px -> 256px; median faces>1 -> "Selected face" + track_identities; brightness<55 -> threshold 0.4; motion<2.5 -> stabilize_face; motion>14 -> temporal_detection (routes_diagnostics.py:~205-250). Heuristic thresholds.
- [E1b] advanced_settings_manager AUTO: face_detector_threshold 0.45 when 4K or >=3 faces, else 0.5; detector_scale_pyramid "on" if >5 faces else "auto" (advanced_settings_manager.py:~1015-1025). FAST preset: detector scrfd_2.5g, threshold 0.55, pyramid off, temporal_step 2 (:806-826).
- [E1b] Quality endpoint: detection_rate % and sharpness via Laplacian var (/800 -> score) (routes_quality.py:~120-130).
- [C2] Detection/pose: temporal_tracker full-frame detect every 8 frames (app/roop/temporal_tracker.py:299), ROI pad stable 0.85 / uncertain 1.35, min ROI 160px (303-305); pre-pass interval K clamp 1..8 default 1 (app/roop/prepass_policy.py:23-26). State machine labels profile at yaw >55° (app/roop/temporal_state_machine.py:365). Stage0 pose bins yaw ±25 / pitch ±15 / roll ±15 (app/roop/stage0_preflight.py:63-72 comments) → 3x3x3 source cells. visibility_alignment rebuilds primary keypoints with visibility <0.50 (app/roop/visibility_alignment.py:41). Association: composite_cost = identity·reliability·(1-sim) + 1·(1-GIoU) + 0.5·kp-dist/size (app/roop/association_cost.py:139-166); tracker.py uses max_cost .85, 0.6 motion + 0.4 identity (app/roop/tracker.py:250-253).

#### (2) 2D warping vs 3D volumetric / faceset / .fsz
- [F] Pipeline is 2D: InsightFace 5-pt similarity alignment (`estimate_norm(kps,112)` in Frame Editor tracked-drawing, `app/ui/tabs/extras_tab.py:927-945`). The only "3D" feature is `use_3d_recon` "🧊 3D source pose matching (experimental)": "Warps the source face to approximate the target head pose before embedding"; uses only insightface `landmark_3d_68` + solvePnP/warpAffine, no volumetric model, no model download (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:78-83`, `app/setup_3drecon.py:1-24`). Default True in app/default_config.yaml:161 but False in settings.schema.json (use_3d_recon).
- [F] "Multi-angle source bank": with multi-image faceset, auto-select source whose pose best matches target frame; needs `.fsz` with front+profile photos (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:84-89`); default True in yaml:164, False in schema.
- [F] `.fsz` = plain zip of PNG face crops (`app/ui/tabs/facemgr_tab.py:174-188` → `util.zip`, output `faceset.fsz`); no 3D data in the format. Faceset upload cap 512 MiB × 64 files; `.fsz` only ext (`app/safe_paths.py:148,153-158`).
- [F] Frontalization: controls exist but are `visible=False, interactive=False`, value False, threshold slider default 30.0 (range 0–90) (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:90-103`); config default `frontalization_threshold 15`, `use_frontalization false` (yaml:39,162). Dead in legacy UI.
- [F] Swap-model dropdown is hard-coded `choices=["inswapper"]`, hidden, non-interactive (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:104-110`) although the backend/config use `hyperswap`/`realswap`; `start_swap` default `swap_model='inswapper'` (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:2299-2300`).
- [F] Models benchmarked in `bench_stages.py` swap stage: inswapper_128 (128→4 tiles @256), reswapper_256, hyperswap_1a/1b/1c_256, ghost_1/3_256, simswap_256, simswap_unofficial_512, blendswap_256, uniface_256, hififace_unofficial_256 (`app/tools/bench_stages.py:80-92`). Pixel-boost tiles = (subsample/output_size)²; per-face cost = tiles × ms/call.
- [E1a] 3D/volumetric: `use_3d_recon` False, `use_source_bank` False, `use_frontalization` False, stereo_mode "MONO_2D", vr_mode False (app/settings.py:1157-1162; schema). No .fsz/faceset volumetric logic in group; faceset routes are in routes_faceset (outside). Pipeline is 2D inswapper-style (`swap_model` default 'realswap', app/settings.py:1163).
- [D] Entire group is 2D: crops are 128/256/512 fixed (app/roop/vram_governor.py:94-112 swap 128, GPEN 256/512/1024/2048, wav2lip 96, musetalk 256); no 3D volumetric / faceset / .fsz code. 1k3d68 appears only as a 68-pt landmark ONNX forced to FP32 (app/roop/precision_policy.py:458). `GpuFaceSwapStage` (app/roop/vectorized_pipeline.py:756) does affine align/reverse-warp on CUDA, but is unwired. LivePortrait warping_spade 5-D GridSample is rewritten into 4-D ops for TRT (app/roop/gridsample5d.py:1-17,238-300).
- [B] Pipeline is purely 2D: 5-pt similarity warp into model crop, swap, inverse-affine paste (BaseFaceSwapper.paste_back/paste_roi; app/roop/processors/frame/swapper_base.py:181-349). No depth, mesh, NeRF, Gaussian splat or .fsz handling in group B.
- [B] Faceset: V2 faceset provides pose-binned embeddings: `pose_embedding_for_target` selects exact (yaw,pitch) cell with fallback=False else identity centroid — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:22-70. Dermal patch from V2 faceset used in legacy swapper (app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1406-1467, resolve_dermal_patch).
- [B] Profile/lateral views: no 3D reconstruction; the code measured that no swapper can rescue a track that never bound a source ("limiter is intake, not the net"), swap rate identical 52.1%/94.8% across models — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:143-168.
- [B] Identity-algebra direction assets (npz+json): only 'jawline' marked writable and weak (jaw ratio -0.011..+0.009); age, gender, expression NOT writable — app/roop/assets/identity_render_validation.json:13,47,81,116.
- [B] Swap model resolutions: inswapper_128 (128, [0,1], emap, B=1 only), reswapper_128/256, hyperswap_1a/1b/1c 256 and 1a/1b/1c 512, alpha_256, ghost_1/2/3 256, simswap_256, simswap_unofficial_512 (512), blendswap_256, uniface_256, hififace_unofficial_256, InStyleSwapper256 A/B/C fp16, cscs_256 (3 files, 1.26 GB), realswap = hyperswap_1a_256 + secondary hififace — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:116-536.
- [E2] Everything in E2 is strictly 2D: live tracked frames are a 2D similarity (partial affine) warp of the previous composite output + hull mask blended with alpha, live_mode.py:~280-330. No volumetric/.fsz/faceset code in this group. `.roop` is JSON session (format `roop-session`, v1), app/roop/project_io.py:23-24; face_bank stored as embeddings list + clusters only, app/roop/project_io.py:117-125.
- [A] NO volumetric/NeRF/mesh/Gaussian/depth data exists in group A. 'face_3d_recon' is PnP + horizontal flip + bounded shear (yaw shear cap 0.20, pitch cap 0.08) on a 2D source crop (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:172-220,250-263); `Face3DRecon.fit_source` returns the crop only and `render_from_coefficients` returns None (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:345-355). Default use_3d_recon False (globals.py:350; app/roop/quality_profiles.py:131 enables it in one profile).
- [A] face_frontalize = global 2x3 affine (cv2.estimateAffine2D RANSAC thr 8, 2000 iters) from 68-pt reprojection at zero rotation; rejects reflection det<=0 (app/roop/face_frontalize.py:123-141). Trigger >25 deg yaw or pitch (174-191).
- [A] .fsz format (faceset_v2.py): ZIP of root-level PNG members `0.png,1.png,...` (legacy contract) + `metadata.json` (FORMAT_NAME 'roop.fsz', version 2); members written with fixed ZipInfo date 1980-01-01, deflate level 6 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:956-1010); atomic temp-file write; SHA-256 per PNG in metadata.integrity (994, 1129-1154); read verifies zip CRC + hashes; legacy archive (no metadata) is accepted and re-detected (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:209-212). `migrate_legacy_fsz` stamps all entries pose_bin 'frontal' without detection (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1164-1200).
- [A] metadata fields: schema, version, source_name, compatibility.legacy_fields_preserved [faces, ref_images, embedding_average, embeddings_backup, face_3d, face_3d_bank, face_poses], identity{arcface_embedding, embedding, normalized_embedding, mean_embedding, aggregation 'quality_weighted_mean_of_pose_specific_embeddings'}, identity_details (16x16 descriptor + high_frequency 64px residual), default_embedding, mean_embedding, pose_bins (3x3 yaw x pitch cells with embedding/members/support), dermal_patch, pose_bank (9-way), sources[] (per-reference: identity.{512-d raw+normalized}, geometry.{bbox, landmarks_68, landmarks_68_3d, landmarks_2d, yaw/pitch/roll, face_scale, facial_proportions}, quality.{9 parts+score}, appearance, expression, identity_details, pose_bin, pose_cell), rejected[], gates{laplacian_floor 100, min_identity_cosine .60, min_quality .35}, index{embedding_metric 'cosine_distance', normalized_embeddings, reference_members} (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:906-948,554-587).
- [A] 3D data in .fsz: only `landmarks_68_3d` = insightface landmark_3d_68 xyz (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:199-203,566). No mesh/depth/SH/texture-atlas. `face_3d`/`face_3d_bank` are in-memory crop caches (app/roop/FaceSet.py:18-22) listed as legacy fields but not written by write_faceset_v2 [INFERENCE from absence in the metadata builder].
- [A] Selection caps: DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES 32, MAX_PER_BIN 6 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:42-43). Quality weights: face_size .18, sharpness .20, det .16, lm .12, exposure .10, saturation .05, occlusion .10 (default 0.75 when not supplied), pose_suitability .09 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:550-553,535-536).
- [A] Load path re-detects each reference PNG and matches cached rows by bbox IoU; geometry/embedding cache is NOT used for the live Face (app/source_gallery.py:259-301). `faceset_manager` returns cached normalized embeddings only for the 'default' model and is unwired (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:24-26).
- [A] Pose-aware source choice: (a) V1/folder: `reference_embedding_for_pose` nearest-two blend (app/roop/face_reference.py:269-299; blend only if second dist^2 within 100 of first); (b) V2: `pose_bin_embedding` 3x3 cell all-or-nothing else default (app/roop/FaceSet.py:168-181); (c) `get_pose_matched_embedding` top-3 inverse-distance SLERP, torch (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1264-1384); (d) `select_reference_index` cost .60 cosine +.25 yaw/45 +.10 pitch/35 +.05 lum (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1387-1414); (e) `pose_source_selector.select_pose_aware_source` temporal cost with hysteresis margin .035 (app/roop/pose_source_selector.py:306-396); (f) `source_portfolio.route` e=normalise(.7*e_profile+.3*e_fused) when |yaw|>35 and profile bin exists (app/roop/source_portfolio.py:326-336).
- [A] `needs_3d` (app/roop/pose_source_selector.py:342-396) set True when reason != 'source_pose_sufficient' (pose_gap>.42, between poses >.12 gap & >10 deg, off_axis>=72, proportion mismatch>.42, perspective risk>.72, inverted, conf<.45); consumer app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:6051-6052 gates use_3d_recon path, i.e. flip/shear only.
- [C1] 2D similarity alignment only: `canonicalize_face_alignment(plate, target_face, subsample_size, swap_template)` (app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5731); template arcface / mtcnn_512 (hififace) / arcface_112 (ghost/simswap): app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5726-5730. model_output_size default 128 (inswapper), subsample_size multiple of it => pixel boost tiles `(subsample/128)^2`: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5718-5724.
- [C1] Pixel boost = slice aligned crop into model-sized tiles, run swap per tile, reassemble (implode/explode): app/roop/procmgr_tiling.py:103-110.
- [C1] "3D" in this repo is only pose-based: `use_3d_recon` option, source bank (multi-angle), frontalization; no volumetric/faceset(.fsz) code in this group (grep). Jaw reshape = 106-pt TPS (indices 0..32) liquify via cv2.remap, off unless enabled: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:262-292.
- [E1b] This group contains only 2D similarity/affine paths (cuda_warp_affine grid_sample bilinear, app/roop/utilities.py:162-215; rotation_affines; profile_alignment_matrix). `globals.use_3d_recon = False` flag exists (globals.py:~335) and core.batch_process_regular accepts `use_3d_recon=False` (app/roop/core.py:1437) – consumer outside group; [INFERENCE] a legacy toggle, no volumetric/.fsz/faceset 3D logic in these 31 files. Facesets referenced only as `INPUT_FACESETS` list (globals.py:323) and `VideoJob.facesets` tuple (app/roop/process_manager.py:33).
- [C2] 2D vs 3D: nothing here is volumetric. light_harmonizer builds a hard-coded ellipsoid+Gaussian depth prior (app/roop/light_harmonizer.py:65-150), not a recon; quality_profiles "ultra" sets `use_3d_recon: True` (quality_profiles.py:~110) but only toggles a flag. vfx_multipass is 2x3 affine → 3x3 + corner pin (app/roop/vfx_multipass.py:22-45). Faceset/.fsz not in this group (aside from final_quality_gate.audit_faceset 134).

#### (3) Visual quality / blending / masks / color / restorers / resolution
- [F] Legacy UI controls (name · default · range): Offset Face Top/Bottom/Left/Right · cfg.mask_* (yaml 0) · 0–2.0 step 0.01; Face Mask Edge Blend · yaml 25 · 0–200; Mouth Mask Top/Bottom/Left/Right · 1 · 0–2.0; Mouth Mask Edge Blend · yaml 10 · 0–200; mask engine dropdown ["None","Clip2Seg","DFL XSeg"] (yaml default `DFL XSeg`); clip text default `cup,hands,hair,banana`; "Restore original mouth area" checkbox; Original/Enhanced blend ratio 0–1 (yaml 1); subsample upscale ["128px","256px","512px"] (yaml 256px); number of swapping steps 1–5 (yaml 1) (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:66-168,245-255`; `app/default_config.yaml:35,49-56,63,68,130,72`).

Mask engines in schema: default `RealityUX` not in legacy dropdown.

Mask models: xseg, xseg_3 (same 430-node network, 2.54 vs 2.75 ms), face_occluder (cheapest 2.31 ms), resnet18 BiSeNet, FastSAM-s, MobileSAM enc/dec (`app/tools/bench_stages.py:61-88,100-120`).
- [F] Config quality knobs: `color_transfer_mode lct`, `color_match_after_enhance true`, `mask_edge_mode gaussian`, `merger_sharpen 0.45`, `merger_hist_match 0.45`, `merger_grain_match 0.75`, `merger_clarity 1`, `codeformer_fidelity 0.55`, `eyes_feather_blend 25`, `restore_original_eyes/mouth false`, `parser_regions [skin,brows,eyes,nose,mouth]`, `expression_restore_strength 0`, `detail_transfer_strength 0`, `swap_model_mask_strength 0`, `upscale_model_after fsr_x2`, `upscale_after_swap false`, `target_conditioned_appearance_* (strength 0.75, alpha 0.3)`, `temporal_compositing_* (strength 0.65, color 0.55, detail 0.86, mask_alpha 0.3, max_feather 8)` (`default_config.yaml`).
- [F] Occluder: `ProcessMgr.initialize` injects `mask_occluder` (256×256 ONNX) LAST in chain (faceswap→ultramax→mask_realityux→mask_occluder), composite = `M_face·(1−M_occ)`; cost 3.79 ms/face vs RealityUX 25.81 ms/face; clean-face damage mean 0.898/255 (noise floor 0.71), swap retained 98.0% (10 frames) (`PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` §3, Measured 2).
- [F] Occluder effect on synthetic texture occluder (peak cover 40.8%, 20 frames): worst 18%→20%, median 40%→42%, failing 7/7 both — "inside the noise, not claimed as improvement"; baseline Phase 10: 8–30% worst, 22–53% median. Real-occluder footage test NOT run (`PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` Still owed 1).

- [D] fused_postprocess (NOT in render): Reinhard LAB with chroma std ratio clamped [0.8,1.2], face = lw*blur(CT(swap)) + (1-lw)*blur(restored) + w*(restored - blur(restored)); defaults detail_weight .75, blur sigma 2.0, feather sigma 2.0, low_weight 1.0; grain from Immerkaer noise sigma; LabStatsEMA alpha .15, cut 18 (measured EMA made pumping worse +9..20%) (app/core/fused_postprocess.py:119-160,371-377).

Odd dims are trimmed (not padded) `w-1`/`h-1` then `scale=`, app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:229-231,350-353. `ROOP_FFMPEG_COLORSPACE=off` disables.
- [E2] Default quality: `FFMPEG_VideoWriter(codec="libx265", crf=14, preset="faster")`, app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:171-172; x264/x265 preset valid set + default 'faster' via `ROOP_ENCODER_PRESET`/`ROOP_RUNTIME_ENCODER_PRESET`, app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:290-296. NVENC: `-rc vbr -cq <crf> -preset p5 -tune hq` (preset default p5 `NVENC_PRESET_DEFAULT`, app/roop/ffmpeg_path.py:47; override ROOP_NVENC_PRESET validated against p1..p7, app/roop/ffmpeg_path.py:50), app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:268-274.
- [E2] Quality clamp: 0–51 (x264/x265/NVENC/QSV), 0–63 for libvpx/vp9/aom/av1/svtav1; default invalid→14, app/roop/util_ffmpeg.py:55-57,197-209. Docs claim libx265 crf 80 / nvenc cq 80 fail outright, util_ffmpeg.py:~44-54.
- [E2] NVENC "quality-first" bundle: p6 + `-multipass fullres` + `-spatial-aq 1` + `-temporal-aq 1` (env ROOP_NVENC_QUALITY_FIRST); PyNvVideoCodec kwargs `multipass`, `aq=1`, `temporalaq=1`, app/roop/nvenc_quality.py:40-119. Default off.
- [E2] finalize_web_video: `h264_nvenc -preset p6 -tune hq -rc vbr -cq 19 -b:v 0`, `-vf pad=ceil(iw/2)*2:ceil(ih/2)*2`, yuv420p, faststart, aac 192k; fallback libx264 `-crf <quality=18>`; ROOP_FINALIZE_NVENC=0 skips NVENC, app/roop/util_ffmpeg.py:67-68,71-116,151-153.
- [E2] Classical upscale: lanczos | fsr(lanczos + `cas=strength` default 0.5 via ROOP_CAS_STRENGTH) | spline(36) | sinc; max output side min(ROOP_UPSCALE_MAX_DIM=8192, NVENC cap h264 4096 / hevc,av1 8192), even dims, app/post_swap.py:412-487. Image path: INTER_LANCZOS4 and for fsr unsharp (GaussianBlur σ=1.0, amount = strength), app/post_swap.py:490-502. AI upscale: up to ROOP_UPSCALE_THREADS=4 (cap 6) workers on a shared session, ordered writeback, app/post_swap.py:150-162. Interp: `rife_2x|rife_4x|minterpolate_2x`, factor clamped 2–4, unknown→rife 2×; minterpolate = `minterpolate=fps=…:mi_mode=mci:mc_mode=aobmc:vsbmc=1`, app/post_swap.py:676-690,~815.
- [E2] Live mode ProcessOptions: blend_ratio default 0.8, distance_threshold 1, subsample_size 256, enhancer forced "None", all stabilize_* False, mask_xseg optional; mask for tracked frames = convex hull of landmarks, GaussianBlur σ=2.0, virtualcam.py:~65-85, live_mode.py:~165-172.

- [E1b] Output-quality scoring weights (routes_quality.py:~140-155): video identity 0.45 / stability 0.30 / sharp 0.15 / detection 0.10; image identity 0.7 / sharp 0.3; identity_score = (sim−0.15)/(0.55−0.15)*100; grades A>=85,B>=70,C>=55,D>=40,else E; samples up to 24 frames.
- [C2] Quality/blending: compositing_engine = linear-light + OKLab photometrics, multiband blend sigma=max(1.5,feather) with feather_px 6.0, unsharp sigma 1.0 strength 0.22 (app/roop/compositing_engine.py:367-453,~600). Mask: mask_fusion alpha .7, sigma 14, hysteresis .38/.60 ±.08, guided radius 4 eps 1e-3 (app/roop/mask_fusion.py:52-88); soft-distance matte margin 8–18px sigmoid k=8 (app/roop/temporal_smoother.py:423-432); temporal_compositing 64x64 mask EMA alpha .30 max_feather 8, off by default. Grain: sigma_added=sqrt(max(σt²−σp²,0)), max 12 (app/roop/grain_match.py:75-78,138). temporal_color LAB EMA with cut restart mean>18 / gain>.35 (app/roop/temporal_color.py:60-61). Profile tables: FAST scrfd_2.5g@320, tracking_freq 2, gpen none, xseg none, batch 16, hyperswap fp16; BALANCED scrfd_10g@512, gpen_256 0.6, xseg "DFL XSeg", mask blend 25, batch 8, mixed; QUALITY retinaface_r50@512, realswap, gpen_256_pro 0.75, "RealityUX", blend 12, batch 4; ULTRA retinaface_r50@640, restore_ultra 1.0, alignment ultra (+3D recon flag), batch 4; all nvdec + nvenc_hevc (app/roop/quality_profiles.py:200-310). AUTO: laptop <7168MB forces enhancer none, libx264 encode (quality_profiles.py:~250-260). Motion blur threshold Var(Laplacian)=100, kernel ≤31 (app/roop/motion_blur.py:29-31).

#### (4) Temporal tracking / smoothing / scene-cut
- [F] Config: `stabilize_method one_euro`, `stabilize_min_cutoff 0.1`, `stabilize_beta 0.1`, `stabilize_face/landmarks/mask/enhancer true` (mask & enhancer strength 0.6), `stabilize_hf_texture true` weight 0.15, `hf_flow_*` (cycle_sigma 1.5, bilateral_sigma 10.0), `temporal_detection true`, `track_identities true`, `track_stitch auto`, `temporal_quality_history 4`, `temporal_quality_control false`, `interp_after_swap off` (`app/default_config.yaml:114-128,144-152`).
- [F] Tracker (PHASE2): Kalman 8-state constant velocity + Hungarian (IoU+ArcFace), `max_age=30`; `MAX_COAST_FRAMES=15`, `MAX_LOST_FRAMES=30`; coasting guards: ≥3 hits, predicted box not >50% off frame, not on another track's real detection, decaying det_score, stamped `_interpolated`; `ROOP_COAST_FRAMES=0` disables; `ROOP_TEMPORAL_GAP=10` interpolation cap; bounded deques for bbox/landmark history; forward+backward pass, young prediction wins; interpolation runs first (`PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` §1).

Optional PySceneDetect ContentDetector thr 27/min 15 (161). Optical flow backend ladder: nvof(cv2.cuda NvidiaOpticalFlow_2_0)→torch LK→cv LK (win15, 3 levels, 20 it, eps .01) (app/roop/flow_backend.py:10-60,262-287); dense mask flow: DIS FAST @128 (app/roop/mask_fusion.py:104,app/roop/occlusion_mask.py:160) with Farneback fallback; smart-skip tiers nvof→cuda_farneback→DIS (app/roop/temporal_smart_skip.py:98); HF flow stabiliser @64 (app/roop/temporal_smoother.py:829). Smart skip: ≤2 consecutive skips, displacement <0.15px, illum Δ<0.5% (app/roop/temporal_smart_skip.py:29-32). TRT usable only if `gpu_preflight` built a TRT session on a tiny ONNX probe (app/roop/backend_manager.py:255-260; app/roop/gpu_preflight.py:166-196). Sub-7 GB cards are NOT excluded from TRT (app/roop/backend_manager.py:117-136); they only get the safety tier (`is_sub_7gb_gpu`: total < 7.0 GB, app/roop/backend_manager.py:111). Triton kernel block 32, MAX_HALO 8 (64x64 tile needs 161 KB smem, Ada has 99 KB) (app/core/fused_postprocess.py:57).

Hetero OpenVINO NPU/iGPU offload for detection/masking/recognition is OFF by default and untested on validated boxes (app/roop/face_analyser.py:893-922).

- [A] Recognition models (app/roop/recognition_registry.py:54-121): default=buffalo_l w600k_r50 (512-d, RGB, 112x112, mean/std 127.5, sha256 4c06341c...), adaface IR101 WebFace4M (512-d, BGR, 112), glintr100/antelopev2 (512-d, same file), mobilefacenet w600k_mbf (512-d), facerecognizersf SFace (128-d, raw BGR). MagFace/CosFace/GhostFaceNet deliberately NOT registered. Swapper always consumes w600k 512-d (app/roop/swap_identity.py:25-26).

CudaOrtIOBinding: per-session reusable torch CUDA buffers, returns None on any failure -> normal session.run (app/roop/utilities.py:41-160).
- [E1b] Small-card gate: VRAM <7.0 GiB total triggers per-session admission by current free VRAM, else model on CPU (app/roop/utilities.py:364-444). release_resources frees analyser, process mgrs, frame cache, zero-copy pools, CUDA cache/ipc/cublas workspaces (app/roop/core.py:629-678). `_admit_vram_governor` is advisory ("render continues ungoverned" on failure) using `CFG.vram_safety_margin_gb` or governor default (app/roop/core.py:1553-1569).
- [E1b] AUTO settings tiers: <1 GB or "cpu" -> CPU (batch 1, margin 0.5); <7 GB or "3060"/"laptop" -> batch 4 (2 if 4K/heavy enhancer), margin 1.0; else batch 16 (8 if 4K/heavy), margin 2.5; nvenc preset p4 for 4K else p5 (advanced_settings_manager.py:~985-1015). `sanitize_settings` clamps unsafe vram_safety_margin to 1.0 (:~930).
- [E1b] process_manager: one `spawn` child per video, strictly sequential (policy: two renderers contend for GPU), poll 0.25 s, join 15 s, terminate on stop; `keep_going` records failures and continues (app/roop/process_manager.py:87-190).

- [F] Legacy `start_swap` → `roop.core.batch_process_regular(output_method, list_files_process, mask_engine, clip_text, in_memory, mask_json, restore_original_mouth, num_swap_steps, progress, …, use_3d_recon, mask_per_frame_json, use_source_bank, use_frontalization, frontalization_threshold, swap_model)` (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:2296-2358`). Update flow: snapshot root `.update-snapshots`, staging `.update-staging`, transaction `.update-transaction.json`; health timeout 900 s (`app/update_manager.py:66-70`). `.gitignore` excludes models/temp/output.

Resume: `run.py --resume` uses project_checkpoint + ffprobe segment verify with `discard_truncated_tail=True` (app/run.py:325-355). Routes: /api/swap, /stop, /pause, /resume, /api/render/preflight, /api/history, /api/profiles, queue/projects/export routers.
- [D] NVDEC: `ffmpeg -hwaccel cuda` raw pipe, default on (ROOP_NVDEC!=0), probe-first with cv2 fallback; bgr24 default, NV12 only ROOP_NVDEC_NV12=1 (NV12 changed detector/tracker decisions, <=6-level colour delta); prefetch 1 if <7 GB else 2 (app/roop/nvdec_reader.py:1-30,57,145-197). `-fps_mode passthrough` (fallback `-vsync 0`) (:65-82). Encoders: optimized_processor h264_nvenc preset p5 default (app/roop/optimized_processor.py:324-327); vectorized_pipeline h264_nvenc p4 CQ 18 bt709 (app/roop/vectorized_pipeline.py:139-148) — differing presets noted in env.py/ffmpeg_path.py. NVENC preset pick: highest-quality preset p1-p7 encoding at >=2x render fps (app/roop/benchmark/autotune.py:208-224). Raw-pipe reader/writer in optimized_processor (:166-517) writes NO frame images. regression harness uses app ffmpeg for raw decode/encode fps (app/roop/benchmark/regression.py:224-300).

- [E2] **Segment resume** (segment_writer.py): segments named `.{base}.seg{NNNN}{ext}` in output dir (hidden), chunk = max(50, ROOP_RESUME_CHUNK) or `round(ROOP_RESUME_SEGMENT_SECONDS=120 × fps)`, manifest `<target>.resume.json` (MANIFEST_VERSION 1): `{version, source(abs), frame_start, frame_end, width, height, fps(3dp), codec, crf, signature, writer_options{preset,bitrate,threads,ffmpeg_params,colorspace,sar,dar,color,lut_path,lut_bake}, [hdr], effective_codec, segments:[{file,frames,bytes,sha256}]}`; written `.tmp`+fsync+os.replace, app/roop/segment_writer.py:51,112,149-330. Resume requires full identity equality (fps tol 0.01), contiguous existing prefix, sha256 match, then ffprobe verify discarding truncated tail only, app/roop/segment_writer.py:265-300,116-146.
- [E2] Close: single segment → `os.replace` promote; else disk-space check (parts size +max(64 MiB,1%)) then `ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i list -c copy [-movflags +faststart]`; failure raises and keeps parts; parity assertion total_encoded == frame_end-frame_start on completed run; ROOP_RESUME_KEEP=1 keeps parts after Stop; ROOP_RESUME=0 disables (docstring), app/roop/segment_writer.py:497-640. Codec change mid-run aborts with IOError (no mixed-codec concat), app/roop/segment_writer.py:400-411.

DEFAULT_UNSEGMENTED_INTERVAL=100 (min 10), app/roop/journal.py:33,290.
- [E2] **Project checkpoint** (project_checkpoint.py): `projects/<id>.json` schema v1: `{schema_version,id(16 hex),job_id,name,state∈PAUSED|INTERRUPTED|RECOVERABLE|FAILED|COMPLETED|PROCESSING,created_at,updated_at,application{compatibility{project_schema:1,processing_contract:"segmented-video-v1"},version},inputs{sources,target,frame_start,frame_end,target_faces,target_context},settings{payload,fingerprint},runtime{provider,precision,models{swap_model,enhancer,mask_engine,mask_engine_2,detector_engine,upscale_model_after,interp_after_swap,recognizer},hardware{signature,profile_key,gpu,vram_tier,vram_gb,ram_gb},platform},output,checkpoint{sequence,safe_frame,next_frame,segments,manifest,manifest_identity,written_at},partial_output{files,integrity},error,stage_cache}`; process-wide RLock; atomic write with 8 replace attempts, app/project_checkpoint.py:36-48,235-307,365-418. - [E2] **Stage cache** (project_checkpoint + stage_cache + stage_checkpoint): basis = {version 1, target sha256+size, trim, source sha256+id, target_faces fingerprint, target_context mapping keys, settings fingerprint, models, provider, precision, live{app_version, sha256 of all non-volatile ROOP_* env}}; sidecars `.stage2.npz` (uncompressed zip, columnar arrays, allow_pickle=False, format "roop-stage-cache" v1) and `.trajectory.npy` (memory-mapped), each verified by size+sha256; Stage1 change invalidates Stage2/trajectory; COMPLETED discards cache; volatile prefixes ROOP_RUNTIME_, ROOP_NVDEC, ROOP_REACT_CLIENT, ROOP_HEADLESS, ROOP_VALIDATION, ROOP_BATCH_SWAP_MAX, ROOP_UPSCALE_TILE, app/stage_checkpoint.py:37-48; app/project_checkpoint.py:483-563.

- [E2] **Segment join for queue splits** (segment_output.py): `{output}/.segments/{group}/seg_{idx:04d}_{start}_{end}.mp4`; join = `ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i manifest [-i source -map 0:v:0 -map 1:a?] -c:v copy -c:a (copy if source audio ∈ aac,mp3,ac3,eac3,alac else aac) -movflags +faststart` to `{stem}_joined.mp4`, no `-shortest`; verify duration tolerance max(0.1, 2/fps) and sum-of-segments 0.5 s, app/roop/segment_output.py:44-45,230-253,312-430.
- [E2] **Audio mux** (util_ffmpeg): untrimmed/trimmed stream-copy path uses `-c:v copy -c:a copy -vsync cfr -fps_mode cfr -avoid_negative_ts make_zero -map_metadata 1`, bounded by `-t min(audio duration, video duration)` (never `-shortest` unless duration unknown) because -shortest dropped the final frame; trimmed cut via `__audio_cut.mka` with AAC-packet lead compensation; offset/other-timing-source path re-encodes aac 192k with `adelay`, app/roop/util_ffmpeg.py:798-806,880-1006.
- [E2] **VFR** (vfr_timing/mp4_timing): VFR if r_frame_rate vs avg_frame_rate differ >0.1% or any frame >1 ms off average-rate grid (ffprobe packet pts); finalize_render then re-stamps intermediate samples at source pts, timescale 90000, error check <1e-4 s, atomic replace; frames not dropped/duplicated; trimmed audio cut exact to ~1 AAC packet only, app/roop/vfr_timing.py:35-36,70-84,172-221, app/roop/mp4_timing.py:25,211-310. range_seek: CFR if pts within 0.2 frame of grid → `cap.set(POS_FRAMES)`; VFR → decode from 0, app/roop/range_seek.py:41,61-95. distributed_render rejects VFR (frame step tolerance max(0.2 ms, 2% of period)), app/roop/distributed_render.py:116-121.
- [E2] **Frame-count/color/audio verification**: frames == trim-implied count (slack 0 with header nb_frames, 1 with duration estimate), colour tags equal intermediate's, audio start within 1 frame and overrun ≤1 frame; deletion of intermediate only after pass; on failure partial dest removed and intermediate kept, app/roop/finalize_output.py:134-208,~320-350.
- [E2] **distributed_render** (CLI `--project --output --chunk-seconds 10 --work-dir`): plan from packets (H.264/HEVC only, CFR only, trim start/end must be K-flag keyframes), chunk edges at keyframes ≥ target_seconds, each slice `-ss -frames:v N -c:v copy -avoid_negative_ts make_zero`, validated (packet count + first NAL IDR 5 / 19,20 + first packet sha256 match), child `.roop` with `skip_audio`, `keep_frames False`; joined `-f concat -c copy`; frame count, duration (tolerance max(2 ms, 1/fps)), stream signature equality checked; audio via `restore_audio`; requires MP4, libx264/h264_nvenc/libx265/hevc_nvenc, clear_output off, In-Memory method; automation keyframes/ramps/mask_parameters unsupported (raises), app/roop/distributed_render.py:93-140,202-250,270-434.
- [E2] NLE: FCPXML 1.10 with asset-clips per scene cut, markers "Scene Cut", face lanes as child clips with note "alpha=straight; codec=ProRes 4444 or PNG sequence"; format hard-coded 1920×1080; EDL `FCM: NON-DROP FRAME`, `AX V C`; DF support only in the unwired `frames_to_timecode`, app/roop/nle_interchange.py:86-150,181-235,282-345.
- [A] processing_selection: immutable snapshot (schema 1) built at request time, snapshotted into queue job, consumed unchanged; signature + invalidation reasons (processing_selection.py header,21,184,204). target_media_state / target_person_state: opaque ids, isolated per-media contexts.
- [A] scanner/sparse_range_scan use cap.grab()/retrieve() sequential decode, never seek (scanner.py header; sparse_range_scan.py header) ; hardware scan path with fallback `_hardware_scan_fell_back@616`.
- [A] source_color.ColorPlan produces ffmpeg video_filter/tag_args so encode matches source matrix/range/tags (app/roop/source_color.py:45-72).
- [A] routes_angle_scan frame indices 0-based; UI 1-based; reads via capturer.get_video_frame(path, idx+1) (app/routes_angle_scan.py:31-32,122-126).
- [C1] frame_capped_queue_depth: max frames in flight 64 (`ROOP_MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT`), depth=(per_thread-1)//2: batch.py:14-38. Unified scheduler bound to <=4 frames in flight: batch.py:30-35.
- [C1] Resume default ON (`ROOP_RESUME=1`): SegmentedVideoWriter with checkpoint callback, resume_frames skip, full-resume finalizes without re-render: batch.py:710-730. Writers: SegmentedVideoWriter / HdrVideoWriter / NVHardwareVideoWriter / FFMPEG_VideoWriter: batch.py:74-80,745-752. PauseController cooperative pause at output-safe boundary: runtime.py:42; ProcessMgr._checkpoint_at_safe_output (1613).
- [C1] Source IO: cv2.VideoCapture with NVDEC/ffmpeg fallback (open_video_capture), animated WebP in memory, HDR managed path, VFR timestamps kept: batch.py:385-430,704. endframe exclusive: batch.py:410. Audio via util_ffmpeg.extract_audio_wav: batch.py:141. Sparse auto-range keyframe prescan (`sparse_auto_range`) for active ranges: batch.py:842-929. Stab chunk RAM caps 8192/4096/1536 MB by total RAM (>=55000/>=28000/else), share 0.75|0.58|0.40: stabilization.py:150-213.
- [E1b] **Queue model** (routes_queue.py): persisted to `queue.json` next to module (:93), atomic write via `.tmp`+`os.replace` (:227-240). JOB_STATES = QUEUED, PREPARING, PROCESSING, PAUSE_REQUESTED, PAUSED, COMPLETED, FAILED, CANCELLED, INTERRUPTED, RECOVERABLE (:97-100); SCHEMA_VERSION 2 (:101); legacy `status` projection pending/running/finished/failed/stopped (:102-113); RUNNABLE_STATES = QUEUED, RECOVERABLE (:121). Runner flags (running/paused/current) are NOT persisted so queue always restarts idle (:227-229). On load, any PREPARING/PROCESSING/PAUSE_REQUESTED/PAUSED (or legacy 'running') job becomes RECOVERABLE with error "interrupted by an app restart; start the queue to retry" + interrupted_at (:204-214); load() then also rewrites any remaining status=='running' to pending (:249-255; effectively dead after normalisation).
- [E1b] Concurrency: ONE runner thread "queue-runner" (daemon), `_generation` token retires stale runners (:1044-1068); strictly sequential, RLock `_lock`. start refuses with 400 (nothing runnable), 409 (single run processing), 409 (benchmark running) (:1046-1062).
- [E1b] Job record: schema_version, id (uuid hex[:12]), target_name, target_media_id, source_index, source_name, source_id, payload, processing_selection (frozen), selection_version, request_id, frame_start/frame_end, segment {group,index,count}, label, status, error, added, started, finished, state, progress{fraction,frames_done,frames_total,fps,eta_s,phase,updated_at}, outputs[], cancel_requested, recoverable, project_id (:346-380). Validation: source_index>=0 int, frame_start/end non-negative ints with end>=start (:382-410). Segment marker requires index in 1..count and count>=2 (:332-343).
- [E1b] Dispatch (_run_one@736): re-validates processing_selection after activating target; project jobs validated via `_validate_project` -> RECOVERABLE with "cannot safely resume: …"; fresh jobs create a project checkpoint (`_create_project`) -> FAILED "could not create processing checkpoint" on error (:840-870); state PROCESSING persisted (:~873); exposes `roop_globals._segment_spec` and `_dashboard_job{job_id,batch_index,batch_count}` (:~880-895). Auto-fallback: if payload/job `auto_fallback` and run errored, retried once with enhancer "None" (:~897-905). Segment custody audit on COMPLETED segments + auto-join when group done (:1010-1030).
- [E1b] Cancel: queued job -> CANCELLED immediately ("cancelled before processing"); current job -> cooperative via `_cancel_requested` + `_stop_current()`; 409 if already completed/failed/cancelled (:1193-1220). `/stop` ends batch and stops current render (:1405-1424).
- [E1b] Resume: `/api/queue/resume` with `project_id` validates checkpoint, verifies segments (`roop.journal.verify_and_recover_segments(..., discard_truncated_tail=True)`), else RECOVERABLE (:1096-1180; projects route :253-288). stage_cache gives Stage1/2 resume (see above). Queue file lives in source tree, not user data dir (:93) — potential permission/portability issue.
- [E1b] Output serving: HTTP Range 206/416 handling, 1 MiB chunks, ETag/version validators, share-delete file handle on Windows (app/routes_output.py:168-300).
- [E1b] FFmpeg: `cfr_video_filter` = `fps=<r>,setpts=N/(<r>*TB)`; CFR fps rejected (->fallback 24.0) if non-finite, <=0 or >240 (app/roop/utilities.py:558-575); ffprobe audio sample-rate probe timeout 10 s (:577-588). Export presets: reel_9x16 1080x1920, portrait_4x5 1080x1350, square_1x1 1080x1080, landscape_16x9 1920x1080; never overwrites (suffix `_{preset}_{n}`) (app/routes_export.py:21-65). extras video path writes mp4v via cv2.VideoWriter then `finalize_web_video` (routes_extras.py:~95-110). core.ensure_video_encoder probes configured encoder, picks fallback via qsv_encode.choose_encoder or aborts (app/roop/core.py:2066-2085); destroy() waits up to 120 s for the encode thread to finalize the file (app/roop/core.py:2099-2135).
- [C2] Queue/video IO: render_policy fixed flags (app/roop/render_policy.py:24-31); hdr_pipeline ffmpeg pipe yuv444p16le read, p010 HEVC/AV1 NVENC encode with 3-frame probe, NVDEC unless 12-bit/422/444 (app/roop/hdr_pipeline.py:405-479,614-623); timeline_tracks audio envelope via ffmpeg PCM (app/roop/timeline_tracks.py:203-265); trajectory_cache persisted to checkpoint, mmap npy (app/roop/trajectory_cache.py:1-47); vfx_multipass 10-bit beauty+matte+crops; preview_provider 2 s deadline, 32 frame LRU.
