### Gaps / stubs / defects found
Per reader group; items tagged `[INFERENCE]` were not confirmed by execution.

#### [A] detect/pose/identity/faceset
- face_3d_recon.Face3DRecon.fit_source returns only the crop and render_from_coefficients returns None 'kept so ProcessMgr references don't break' (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:340-355): stubs; the feature named '3D recon' is 2D flip/shear. Docstring says 'no additional model needed' (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:30). There is NO volumetric/3D face model, NeRF, mesh, FLAME/3DMM or depth anywhere in group A.
- Pose convention hazards: solve_pose_5pt returns (yaw, pitch, roll) but app/roop/face_bank.py:244,359 unpacks `pitch, yaw, roll = solve_pose_5pt(kps)` and stores 'yaw': yaw / 'pitch': pitch -> yaw and pitch are SWAPPED in Face Bank entries (offaxis_deg is symmetric so off_axis is unaffected). routes_facemgr._facemgr_ingest treats insightface `face.pose` as (yaw,pitch,roll) (app/routes_facemgr.py:131-132) though insightface order is (pitch,yaw,roll) (app/roop/head_pose.py:53-54; app/roop/faceset_v2.py:219-223 converts correctly) -> when landmark_3d_68 pose exists, Face Manager radar/sharpness FIQA pose and written yaw/pitch are permuted. FaceSet.ensure_face_poses uses a different EPnP decomposer (frontal ~+/-170) vs solve_pose_5pt (app/roop/FaceSet.py:250-256 docs); app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:16-18 itself notes prose in the tree contradicting the sign convention (face_3d_recon says pitch+ = up, nonfrontal says +30 = tilted up).
- face_analyser.adaptive_alignment_matrix / profile_stable_anchor_alignment implement a 3-point profile solver but canonicalize_face_alignment explicitly replaced profile templates with plain `estimate_norm` because they doubled features (app/roop/face_analyser.py:699-722); profile_aware_umeyama_alignment is still used by app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1954 -> two alignment policies coexist.
- face_util _rescue_upscaled is gated by rescue_small_faces default False (globals.py:287): small-face rescue is off by default; first-pass detector at 640 (forced for scrfd/r50) can miss small faces in 4K.
- Detector thresholds: global default face_detector_threshold 0.50 (globals.py:59) vs fallback 0.60 hard-coded in analyser prepare (app/roop/face_util.py:267) vs capture_seed MIN_DET_SCORE 0.6 vs face_bank min_det_score .55 vs adaptive min_confidence .55 vs sparse scan floor .25 (many inconsistent hard-coded floors).
- geometric_alignment.py: module-level `from face_engine.pipeline.stereo import ...` is a hard import of an out-of-tree/optional package (app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:630); module only used by hyperswap_optimizer; `LandmarkGeometrySanity.validate_landmarks` inverted-axis branch is `pass` (dead check, app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:85-88).
- adaptive_detector/face_analyser.TemporalFaceDetector are two overlapping temporal-detection schedulers (adaptive: interval 8, coast 4; temporal: interval 5, max_age 15) with different scene-cut hist implementations; [INFERENCE] only one is wired per path.
- Recognition selection is NOT wired to live identity matching (ui_recognition.SCOPE_NOTE; app/roop/face_analyser.py:1497-1510; app/roop/faceset_manager.py:24-26 'no render consumer yet'). AdaFace off by default (ROOP_ADAFACE) with uncalibrated 0.5 cosine-distance threshold (app/roop/recognizer_adaface.py:21-27,66). Swapper hard-locked to 512-d w600k (swap_identity.py).
- faceset V2 does not cache usable runtime geometry: loader re-detects every PNG (app/source_gallery.py:259-301), so V2 'cached analysis' only helps selection metadata, not load cost; metadata occlusion defaults 0.75 when no occlusion given (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:535-536) so quality score has a built-in constant.
- .fsz has no embedding-model tag (identity.embedding_model absent in written archives; app/roop/faceset_manager.py:21-22) and no 3D mesh/texture; migrate_legacy_fsz stamps every entry 'frontal' (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1187) without detection => legacy pose bank is fictional until re-saved.
- landmark_refresh default OFF (app/roop/landmark_refresh.py:45) despite measured improvement (median 1.21%->0.58% IOD error).
- routes_facemgr: module-global singleton state (not per-session/multi-user); build silently falls back to legacy zip if V2 writer raises (app/routes_facemgr.py:383-390), losing metadata; _facemgr_ingest swallows pose-set errors to zeros (136-141).
- source_gallery.estimate_face_pose_from_kps uses crude ratio thresholds (.65/1.55 yaw, .65/1.45 pitch) while the rest of the tree uses solve_pose_5pt: gallery pose labels can disagree with swap-time routing.
- face_bank.py solve_pose_5pt(kps) may return None -> tuple-unpack TypeError (app/roop/face_bank.py:244,359) (solve_pose_5pt returns None on degenerate input, app/roop/face_util.py:2955-2961).
- Hard-coded limits that fail silently: yoloface fixed 640 (guard added after measured 0% recall at 512, app/roop/yoloface.py:62-95); r50 static TRT profile ignores det_size (app/roop/retinaface.py:56-62); faceset MAX_MEMBER_BYTES 64 MiB skip (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:42); scan step widening at 9000 frames; angle shortlist 40/bin and gaps 10-deg cells cap rare angles at 10 frames (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:342).
- Identity thresholds differ by module: 0.65 (face_distance_threshold default cosine DISTANCE, app/roop/face_analyser.py:795-796), 0.65 DEFAULT_SIMILARITY (cosine similarity, app/roop/scanner.py:44), 0.65 DEFAULT_MIN_COSINE reference clustering (app/roop/face_reference.py:27), .62/.50 dual (similarity), identity_manager gate 0.45 (cost), bystander .42/.22/.12, faceset_v2 .60 outlier, identity_algebra .80 guard; units (distance vs similarity) mixed -> mis-tuning risk (docstring app/roop/face_analyser.py:777-785 explicitly warns).


#### [B] swap/enhancers/masks
- **POST /api/enhancer/ultramax/compile is simulated**: _compile_engine_task only asyncio.sleeps (0.3+0.5+0.3 s) and publishes a fake engine_path models/trt_cache/ultramax_<prec>_<prov>.plan; docstring says "Simulate or execute" — app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:420-460. Initial compilation state is hard-set to ready/1.0 (app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:237).
- **tile_diffusion.py "latent diffusion" is not diffusion**: builtin path does bilinear 8x down, 4th channel = RGB mean, Laplacian update with alphas [0.85,0.5,0.2,0.05]*0.12, bicubic up, 0.25/0.75 mix; CPU path is Gaussian(1.5)+Laplacian boost 1.35. No UNet/VAE. Real pipeline only if app/models/diffusion/<sd-turbo> exists (diffusers). Docs claim "few-step latent diffusion", "2-6 FPS" — app/roop/tile_diffusion.py:419-470.
- **Wav2Lip falls back to a hand-drawn ellipse mouth** (cavity/teeth/tongue ellipses) when ONNX fails; comment says input [-1,1] but code uses /255 [0,1]; model URL github.com/primefaces/roop-models (unverified host) — app/roop/processors/Lipsync_Wav2Lip.py:24,175-262.
- **Legacy frame/face_swapper.py** (2269 lines, EAR/mouth/SH/grain) is not on the render path (only tests + scene_detector.clear_temporal_state import it); has silent no-op fallback stubs for pose/Umeyama/CUDA warp (return 0.0/None/identity) — app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:255-292. Docstring EAR<0.21 vs const 0.18.
- processors/face_swapper.py (TensorRTFaceSwapper), processors/face_enhancer.py (GPENEnhancer) and processors/frame/temporal_stabilizer + color_matcher have no production importers outside onnx_swappers/tests; GPENEnhancer/TRT swapper raise RuntimeError without CUDA.
- hififace verify_tol=0.65 calibrated on one synthetic clip; self-declared "re-measure on real footage" — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:231-233.
- Measured negative results shipped as options: CSCS/InStyleSwapper worse than realswap on real video; only identity intake limits profile swaps — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:96-168.
- hyperswap_optimizer.get_vram_telemetry invents defaults total 12288/free 8192 MB when NVML/torch unavailable (app/roop/hyperswap_optimizer.py:138-142); NVML init at import.
- MAR 5-pt fallback returns constant 0.10 (below 0.18 trigger so mouth restore never fires on 5-pt-only faces) — app/roop/enhancers/mouth_dynamics.py:124-125.
- Enhance_DMDNet loads './models/DMDNet.pth' relative to CWD (not resolve_relative_path) — app/roop/processors/Enhance_DMDNet.py:275; module-level torch/torchvision imports hard-require torch.
- Enhance_KEEP is EXPERIMENTAL and silently passes frames through when sidecar missing — app/roop/processors/Enhance_KEEP.py:10-12.
- xseg3_optimizer docstring names ConfidenceAwareMaskRefiner class but code has function refine_xseg3_mask (docs/code drift) — app/roop/xseg3_optimizer.py:11 vs 141.
- vr_util.get_perspective destructures img.shape as [orig_width, orig_height] (actually h,w) — app/roop/vr_util.py:12.
- model_registry omits inswapper, ghost, simswap etc. from DEFAULT_REGISTRY (only hififace, hyperswap*, alpha, reswapper*): those fall through to FaceSwapInsightFace.SWAP_MODELS legacy path — app/roop/processors/frame/model_registry.py:141-165.
- Duplicate keys in SWAP_MODELS (hyperswap/hyperswap_1a/hyperswap_256 -> same file; reswapper/reswapper_256) — app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:133-247.
- Hard limits: Frame_Filter C64 fixed 320x200; frame_masking fixed 1024; TemporalMatrixStabilizer max_gap=1 resets track on any skipped frame (round-robin workers); ModelRegistry unknown name -> FALLBACK_SWAP_MODEL 'inswapper' (128px).


#### [C1] ProcessMgr+procmgr
- No NotImplementedError/TODO/stub in this group (grep for stub|placeholder|NotImplemented returned zero hits).
- 3D is not present: pose-gated heuristics only (frontalization, source bank, jaw TPS); no volumetric/faceset path.
- Several features are measured-negative and kept OFF by default: bystander veto, flow-align, hi-res miss redetect, ROI-crop, single-person veto (0), non-frontal mask routing (0), mask fusion, occlusion fast-restore.
- Doc/code tension: process_face comment documents a reverted refusal of synthetic (gap-filled/coasted) faces; synthetic faces are now swapped (app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5601-5620) — can paint onto background if coast guards miss.
- Hard-coded: STALE=15, IOU_MIN=0.2 are literals not env (tracking.py:428); stitching/veto numbers tuned on a few clips (comments cite d6, Love/Weeds, Taras).
- Scene-cut thresholds are not in this group (delegated to roop.scene_detector) [INFERENCE: none verified here].
- ProcessMgr.py is a 7654-line monolith (process_face ~2000 lines) with `_swallowed` broad except fallbacks throughout (many silent degrade paths).
- Config coupling via roop.globals getattr defaults and ~60 env knobs makes defaults hard to audit.


#### [C2] temporal/masks/color/tracking
- light_harmonizer docstring says "Neural environment re-lighting" (app/roop/light_harmonizer.py:1) but implementation is classical (ellipsoid prior + bilateral + LS light fit); no neural model, no real 3D.
- quality_profiles "ultra" 3D recon is just a settings flag `use_3d_recon` (quality_profiles.py:~110); profile names `hyperswap`/`realswap`/`RealityUX` are labels mapped to settings, not verified here.
- Kalman kps stabilizer is not default and measured no better than One-Euro on real footage (app/roop/one_euro.py:395-412 docstring); defaults tuned on a single static clip.
- Two divergent scene-cut implementations with different defaults (ratio 6.0/window 61/cold .16 in one_euro.py vs 4.5/45/.15 in scene_detector.py) — duplicate heuristics, both histogram-based (2D appearance only).
- Opt-in modules default OFF: temporal_identity, temporal_occlusion, temporal_compositing, temporal_quality (see rows) — quality depends on config enabling them.
- final_quality_gate/stage17_audit/regression_audit are contracts/documentation: no measured gate thresholds; PREVIOUS_PHASES all `quality_status: evidence_pending` (app/roop/final_quality_gate.py:63-72); stage17 baselines come from SESSION_LOGS.md text, not computed.
- trajectory_cache explicitly does not feed the swap alignment (docstring 1-47): smoothed 112px matrices only for HUD/checkpoint.
- EpipolarStereoTracker (app/roop/temporal_state_machine.py:877) delegates to an external coupler and is only meaningful for stereo input.
- Hard-coded limits: tracker max_lost 30 / coast 15; state machine travel 45px/frame (fails on very fast low-res motion); MAX_SUBSTITUTED 8 frames in flow chain; roto MAX_POLYGONS 24 / points 512; mouth audio offset ±500ms; motion blur kernel ≤31; trajectory series points ≤5000.
- NVOF backend unverifiable in tests (`pragma: no cover`, app/roop/flow_backend.py:214).
- sam2_runs.py is only range arithmetic (no SAM2 model code in this file).


#### [D] compute/runtime
- **Dead gate in optimizer**: `SearchSpace.cudnn_probe_state` looks for `cudnn_algo.lowered_models/downgraded_models/unsafe_models` (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:696-712) but cudnn_algo.py defines none of them (symbols: record, known_algo, apply_algo, has_cuda_ep, probe), so the cuDNN axis is always reported "healthy" and the guard against re-enabling a rejected mode is inoperative.
- **session_pool docs vs code**: module header says pooling is opt-in "default unset keeps single session byte-for-byte" (app/roop/session_pool.py:12-15) but `_auto_pool_defaults` returns 2/2 (12 GB) and 4/4 (>=15.5 GB) automatically (:100-103); docstring table says ">= 15.5 GB -> 8/8" (:82) while code returns 4,4.
- **precision_policy comment contradiction**: header says INT8/FP8 "represented ... a TensorRT feature flag alone does not enable either" (app/roop/precision_policy.py:39-41) yet `_IMPLEMENTED_PROVIDER_PRECISIONS={'bf16','fp8'}` (:42) lets FP8 through for face_swap; runtime_optimizer.PrecisionSelector rejects anything but bf16 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1207-1215) so the two layers disagree on FP8.
- **Swapper FP16 contradiction** (see axis 3): policy says safe/candidate, bench + predictor say FP32-forced.
- **runtime_state._surgeon_info hard-codes** `surgical_models=[xseg,2d106det,1k3d68,restoreformer_pp,codeformer]` (runtime_state.py:~345) while PRECISION_OVERRIDES forces xseg/2d106det/1k3d68/RF++ to plain fp32 and the surgeon is invoked only by face_engine/core/trt_compiler.py:278 — UI/diagnostics imply surgical mixed precision is active when the ORT render path does not use it.
- **Unwired modules** (code present, no render caller): fused_postprocess.py (docstring "Not wired into the render"), l2_persistence.py, vectorized_pipeline.py (+hardware_streamer ring, optimized_trt_engine, RetinaFaceDecoder, GpuFaceSwapStage), inference_optimizer.py, unified_runtime_scheduler.py, trt_engine.py, trt_session_builder (only optimized_*/verify), pipeline_concurrency (only an _ACTIVE_ENGINE probe in app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:385), worker_pool.py ("NO RENDER USES THIS"), optimized_prepass (live path is procmgr_tracking).
- **Synthetic/simulated benchmarks**: inference_optimizer.benchmark_mode fabricates latencies (CPU x3.5, TRT FP16 x0.55 + 1.2 ms dispatch for small models, sin-jitter, vram 128.0 MB constant, notes "Light/Synthesized benchmark profile") when the live path is unavailable (app/roop/inference_optimizer.py:735-760); pipeline_concurrency benchmarks MockStageExecutor, not real stages (app/roop/pipeline_concurrency.py:355-490); bench.py feeds synthetic tensors and models detect post-processing as constants (app/roop/bench.py:36-43).
- **Inconsistent VRAM ceilings across modules**: governor 90% ceiling (app/roop/vram_governor.py:59), runtime fraction .90, optimizer admission .82 / balanced .80 (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:122-124), BottleneckAnalyzer VRAM-bound 85% (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:1079), scheduler margin .12 (=88%), unified_runtime_scheduler 4070 safe 9728/12288 (79%) and 3060 4915/6144 (80%) (app/roop/unified_runtime_scheduler.py:176-196). Different layers may admit/reject the same state.
- **Hard-coded two-GPU assumptions**: pipeline_concurrency profiles (RTX 4070 12 GB / 3060 6 GB), unified_runtime_scheduler HardwareCapacity (12288 / 6144 MB, 24/8 cores), inference_optimizer name match "3060"/"laptop"/"4070"/"ada" (app/roop/inference_optimizer.py:166-170); hardware_validation accepts only the two named targets (app/roop/hardware_validation.py:18). A 3050/4060/5090 maps to generic buckets only by VRAM.
- **TRT cache location mismatch**: strict builder defaults to relative `models/trt_cache` from CWD (app/roop/trt_session_builder.py:334,375) while routes_trt_cache clears `app/models/trt_cache` (app/routes_trt_cache.py:45-46) and vectorized/verify use `models/trt_cache`; engines built from another CWD are invisible to the cache UI and survive 'clear'.
- **Sub-7 GB hard limits**: pools forced to 0, explicit pool values rejected (app/roop/session_pool.py:176-209); batch 1, in-flight 1 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1648-1660); NVDEC disabled in pipeline_concurrency 3060 profile (app/roop/pipeline_concurrency.py:243) but nvdec_reader merely drops prefetch to 1 (app/roop/nvdec_reader.py:190-197); inconsistent.
- **OpenVINO untested on target machines** per docstring (app/roop/openvino_runner.py:17-20); int8 requires >=8 real recorded samples at the dominant shape, no synthetic path.
- **INT8 never selected via ORT**: int8/fp8 flags are probed but policy forces fp32 (app/roop/precision_policy.py:397-400); INT8/FP8 only exist in the separate native trt_quant path for the swapper, which needs a calibration set built by tools/build_calibration_set.py else raises CalibrationSetMissing and falls back (app/roop/trt_quant.py:35-39).
- **Heuristic fallbacks**: _fallback_landmarks from fixed bounding-box fractions (app/roop/optimized_prepass.py:206-226); quality_metrics profile/occlusion judged from synthetic ground truth flags; regression changed-face metric = MAD>4.0 (heuristic).
- **verify_trt_fps / benchmark_speed / benchmark_comparison** are single-model transport benchmarks (not end-to-end quality), exit codes only for verify_trt_fps (2 error, 3 NaN/Inf).
- **PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF set at import** of windows_runtime_compat with max_split_size_mb 128 and expandable_segments False (app/windows_runtime_compat.py:21) — global side effect, ordering-dependent if torch already imported.
- **Not verified (no execution allowed)**: [INFERENCE] that core.py (outside group) is where HEURISTIC is set and where ORT CUDA EP options (gpu_mem_limit, arena strategy) are composed; those EP option values are NOT in group D files, so CUDA-EP defaults (arena, gpu_mem_limit_gb) cannot be tabulated here other than the axes enumerated in app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:635-760 and legacy app/roop/trt_engine.py:81-86.


#### [E1a] api/run/settings
- No NotImplementedError/TODO/stub in group; "stubword" hits are the word in comments only (index flags).
- `--server_share` consumed by api_access via sys.argv but absent from run.py's argparse (app/run.py:164-228) but defined in app/roop/core.py:142; run.py's `parser.parse_args()` (strict) runs first, so it likely errors [INFERENCE; verify].
- No tilt/pose-based refusal thresholds in API: pose is a coarse keypoint log-ratio coverage heuristic (app/api.py:2032-2042), no solved yaw/pitch/roll degrees, no failure threshold for extreme angles; 3D/volumetric settings (use_3d_recon, use_source_bank, use_frontalization, stereo_mode) default off and appear as flags only in this group.
- ENV `mib_to_bytes` ignores env precedence (app/settings.py:268-271 flagged by author as candidate fix).
- Many settings read at import time need restart; only LIVE_ENV_SETTINGS (15 keys) re-exported on save (app/settings.py:212-217).
- `perf_stab_chunk_mb`/`perf_stab_streaming` config_only in schema (no UI).
- Contradictory comments: app/settings.py:11 comment shows pseudo-code; app/run.py:343-352 duplicate headless-project guards (two identical `if` blocks).
- `ROOP_API_PORT` non-integer in run.py:`int(os.environ.get("ROOP_API_PORT","8001"))` has no try/except (crashes) while api.run_api falls back to 8001.
- TOKEN_PAGE is wired: HTML 401 served from app/api.py:7379 for non-API paths; /api,/ws get JSON 401.
- Two thread-derivation paths (load-time knee vs resolve_threads tiers) differ (knee 8/10/20 vs caps 8/12/16).


#### [E1b] routes/core/telemetry
- advanced_settings_manager.resolve_auto_settings: `settings["temporal_step"] = 1 if context.fps > 45 else 1` – both branches equal (advanced_settings_manager.py:~1035); AutoSettingsContext defaults hard-code 30 fps / 1080p / face_count 1.
- core.limit_resources macOS `max_memory * 1024 ** 6` (core.py:~618) likely wrong unit.
- core.parse_args: `--execution-provider` documented as choices but no `choices=`; `--enable-occlusion-mask` default True makes the enable flag a no-op (app/roop/core.py:147,152).
- core.run: project-action branch executes twice-guarded (before and after pre_check) so export/EDL skip model pre_check but `--render` does not (app/roop/core.py:2262-2272) — intentional but undocumented in help.
- No 3D/volumetric/.fsz code in this group; only 2D similarity warps (app/roop/utilities.py:323-362); `use_3d_recon` is a default-False global (globals.py:~335).
- routes_livecam.livecam_sources returns hard-coded "Default Camera (Device 0)" / "Secondary Camera (Device 1)" without probing (app/routes_livecam.py:44-47); `_progress` injected by api.py (None at import) -> start route would TypeError if called unwired (:84-90).
- routes_extras: `_FRAME_COLORIZERS/_FILTERS/_UPSCALERS` default None; `get_frame_ops` returns null until api.py injects (app/routes_extras.py:37-39,155); video path decodes via per-frame `get_video_frame(path, i)` random access (slow O(n) seeks) and writes mp4v at client-supplied `fps` (default 30.0), ignoring source fps in `/api/extras/apply` (:~47,95-102).
- routes_extras crop/resize: percent crop; `resolution` parse errors silently swallowed (:~66).
- routes_output.list_output hard-limits to 50 files after reverse-name sort, not mtime sort (app/routes_output.py:37-49) — newest files may be omitted when names do not sort chronologically.
- routes_queue.load(): second pass `status=="running" -> pending` is dead after `_normalize_loaded_job` already converts to RECOVERABLE (app/routes_queue.py:249-255). queue.json stored beside source file (:93). Auto-fallback retry only drops enhancer; no other degradation ladder.
- routes_quality: metrics are crude (Laplacian variance /800, cosine-sim linear map 0.15..0.55) and identity needs src embeddings (routes_quality.py:~110-130).
- routes_diagnostics: advisor is rule-based heuristic and returns 409 "busy processing" during renders (:~125); update check shells to git network each 60 s max (cached) and `apply_channel` hard-coded "pinokio" (:568-641).
- routes_telemetry/frames/pipeline_status are decoupled from the pipeline by injected callables (`progress_snapshot`, `frame_source`) – None default means silent empty hello frame / failures if api.py wiring is missing (app/routes_telemetry.py:278,293-299; app/routes_frames.py:113).
- process_manager is sequential only and only a library: [INFERENCE] queue path (routes_queue) uses in-process `_run_swap`, not IsolatedVideoBatch, so crash isolation applies only where the caller uses it.
- stage_profiler H2D/D2H transfer bytes are never measured ("unavailable") (stage_profiler.py:~85).
- terminal_dashboard LATENCY_COLUMNS has 8 columns while stage_telemetry OPS_COLUMNS has 6 (dashboard doc says "seven columns", :~85) – doc/code mismatch.
- pipeline_manager.py contains no logic (pure delegation to AnatomicalEngine) — baseline facade only.
- env.py documents ~260 ROOP_* flags and historic default divergence (ROOP_NVENC_PRESET p4 vs p5, ROOP_OPT_STRICT_TRT) (app/roop/env.py:6-20) — evidence that defaults are still scattered across modules (core.py reads `os.environ.get` directly instead of env_* helpers at ~276-415).


#### [E2] ffmpeg/video-IO/persistence
- desktop_capture.py: docstring promises DXGI/ScreenCaptureKit/XComposite/PipeWire; actual = GDI `BitBlt SRCCOPY` only (Win). `_capture_darwin` returns None and Linux enumerate/capture are stubs (app/roop/desktop_capture.py:373-381); `capture_frame` then SILENTLY substitutes a synthetic animated test frame (`_capture_synthetic_fallback`, 428-466) – on mac/Linux or any Win GDI failure the live pipeline "swaps" a fake desktop with no error. Enumeration fallback returns fake windows ("OBS Studio 30.2", hwnd 1001/1002).
- video_io_hw.py: `open_reader`, `open_writer`, `NvZeroCopyReader`, `NvZeroCopyWriter`, `CpuFallbackWriter` have no callers outside the file (grep over app/); only `open_keyframe_reader`/`NvSparseKeyframeReader`/telemetry are consumed (app/roop/face_bank.py:202, app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:2627). The "zero-copy NVDEC/NVENC" main pass is effectively unused; production decode is the `ffmpeg -hwaccel cuda` pipe (video_stream.py) with host bgr24 copies, encode is the ffmpeg NVENC pipe. QSV decode stated UNVERIFIED on hardware (app/roop/video_io_hw.py:29-31).
- video_io_optimizer.py: whole module (profiler, registry, StreamPreservationSpec) has zero production callers; its `StreamPreservationSpec` uses preset p4/cq14, `-c:a copy` into mp4 (can fail for non-mp4-copyable audio) and `-r {fps:.4f}` (not exact rational).
- Inconsistent encoder defaults: FFMPEG_VideoWriter defaults libx265 crf 14/preset faster (app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:171); NVHardwareVideoWriter defaults hevc_nvenc, preset p4, cq 19 (app/roop/video_stream.py:529-533) vs ffmpeg_writer NVENC p5; finalize uses p6/cq19; probe_encoder hard-codes p5 and cq=crf (app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:64); video_io_optimizer p4. Comment at app/roop/ffmpeg_path.py:38-46 says these were unified, but video_stream and optimizer still differ. NVHardwareVideoWriter takes `cq=19` and `crf` separately—check which wins.
- journal.py: docstring says it verifies `seg_XXXX.mp4` but real segments are `.{base}.segNNNN{ext}`; `JournalManager` (incl. `recover_on_resume`, unsegmented ticks) has no app callers. `segment_writer._write_journal` writes a single `journal.json` into the OUTPUT dir (one file shared by every job rendering into that dir) and `project_checkpoint.update_checkpoint` writes journals with `seg_dir=projects/<id>` where the segments do not live, so `verify_and_recover_segments` on that journal would see missing files and break the chain (inference from app/roop/journal.py:179-230 + app/project_checkpoint.py:395-416).
- journal `probe_segment_with_ffprobe`: a header-only valid file returns (True, 0) and the caller then trusts the manifest's frame count (app/roop/journal.py:171-176,198) – a corrupt-but-parsable tail with wrong frame count is accepted; only the last segment is discarded on failure and earlier bad segments just truncate the chain.
- segment_writer.py: segments always use `FFMPEG_VideoWriter`/NVHardwareVideoWriter per segment ⇒ every segment starts with its own IDR (GOP reset); chunk default 120 s; no per-segment audio. Manifest identity includes `writer_options` containing color plan objects – any change in these invalidates resume (by design). A stale `.resume.json` with changed `hdr` key is discarded (app/roop/segment_writer.py:268-273).
- project_io.py: each `save_project` appends the entire document as a JSONL snapshot to `.roop.journal`, never compacted/truncated – unbounded growth with 120 s autosave on long sessions (app/roop/project_io.py:164-194); `recover_project` takes newest `updated_at >=` file stamp, overwriting the main file.
- project_render.py: `_ensure_checkpoint` returns an existing project checkpoint by `_safe_id` without comparing it to the edited `.roop` document → a re-opened modified `.roop` renders with stale checkpoint settings (app/roop/project_render.py:82-90) [INFERENCE from code path].
- distributed_render.py: no resume across chunks (`chunk workspace is not empty` raises), no retry of failed chunk, single-pass failure raises after all workers finish; GOP-only ≥ keyframe granularity so trims must sit on keyframes (hard error); H.264/HEVC sources only; VFR rejected, contradicting vfr_timing support elsewhere; only one worker thread per GPU (no multi-chunk concurrency per card); automation lanes unsupported.
- nle_interchange.py: CMX3600/OTIO/timecode helpers and `catalog_active_face_timecodes` are never called by the app (grep); `face_segments` is only read from `record["timeline"]` (app/roop/project_io.py:103) and app/api.py:913 sets `record["timeline"] = {"scene_cuts": …}` only, so FCPXML/EDL face lanes are empty unless hand-edited. `_timecode` rounds fps with NDF (no 23.976/29.97 drop-frame) and FCPXML format is hard-coded 1920×1080 regardless of the clip; `catalog_active_face_timecodes` defaults to fake range `[(0,100)]` and OTIO defaults `total_frames` to 1800 when unknown (nle_interchange.py:~250,~425).
- util_ffmpeg.py: `join_videos` non-simple branch passes shell-quoted strings as single args (line ~275-285) – broken; `extract_frames`/`create_video` pass both `-vsync cfr` and `-fps_mode cfr` (newer ffmpeg may reject/ warn on both) [INFERENCE]; `cut_video` seeks with `format(start_time, ".2f")` (10 ms precision, not frame-exact, ignores VFR) and no `-avoid_negative_ts`.
- ffmpeg_writer.py: `print(str(cmd))` of the full command on every spawn (line ~417); odd sizes lose one pixel row/col via scale instead of padding; bgr24 rawvideo (8-bit) pipe means 10-bit plans still ingest 8-bit data.
- live_mode.py: every keyframe runs the full `swap_frame` BEFORE `detect_faces` (second detection) – double detection cost on keyframes; tracked frames warp the previous *composited* output (not the raw swap) so errors/blur accumulate across up to 5 tracked frames; `track.output = self.last_output` shared by all faces so multi-face alpha blending applies the same whole-frame warp per face; no temporal landmark smoothing; capture thread no sleep for webcams; desktop capture polling `time.sleep(1/fps)` ignores capture time; frame size mismatch with virtual camera (camera not honoring resolution) raises in `pyvirtualcam.send`.
- virtualcam.py: no enhancer in live mode (`selected_enhancer="None"`), stabilize options all off; RGB fmt assumed available.
- StreamWriter.py: busy-wait spin loop in `LimitFrames` burns a CPU core; `pyvirtualcam.Camera` raises rather than returning None, so the "No virtual camera found!" branch is dead.
- template_parser.py: `{i}` returns int from `data.get("index")`; `str.replace` with an int raises TypeError (app/roop/template_parser.py:5-26) unless caller passes string.
- watchdog.py: detection only (no remediation/abort); `check_tensor` swallows its own errors (returns True); mask-jump check limited to consecutive frames per (stream, thread, shape) key; baseline dict cleared at >512 keys.
- capturer.py: HEVC non-4:2:0 forced to pipe; ffprobe timeouts 30 s, fallback BMP extract 10 s; frame cache keyed on mtime not content; global capture lock. Fallback seek `ts = target / fps` for VFR is inexact (capturer.py:~510).
- finalize_output.py: slack of ±1 frame when frame count derived from duration; VFR retime errors never fail the render (report only); `stopped` renders skip trim parity; helper-file list covers only `.__audio_cut.mka` and `.resume.json.tmp`.
- post_swap.py: docstring states function bodies were moved byte-for-byte with no test coverage; shared globals (`_progress`, `_make_frame_processor`) injected at import time by api.py – fragile. AI upscale opens `cv2.VideoCapture` on the swapped output (CFR assumption; VFR retime already applied would be lost since it re-encodes via FFMPEG_VideoWriter at `fps*scale`).
- provenance.py: pure re-export; actual watermark/signing in face_engine.pipeline.provenance (not in this group); `enable_provenance_signer` default True, `embed_provenance_watermark` default False.
- keep_awake.py/win_asyncio_compat.py: Windows-only, no-ops elsewhere; keep_awake docstring requires acquire/release on same thread.
- crash_autopsy.py/debug_snapshot.py: diagnostics only; debug_snapshot docstring (4-panel row) vs 2×2 grid code.

Actual argparse flags in the codebase (e.g. `--disable-occlusion-mask`, referenced `PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` §4) live in `roop/core.py` (outside group F). Run.py perf env mapping not visible here.
- `default_config.yaml` is not a defaults file: it contains machine-specific `hardware_signature`, `_threads_basis v3|24|10`, `benchmark_results {}`; and contradicts the schema defaults (swap_model hyperswap vs realswap; selected_enhancer `Restore Ultra` vs `UltraMax`; mask_engine `DFL XSeg` vs `RealityUX`; face_detector_size '512' vs 640; use_3d_recon/use_source_bank true vs false; track_stitch, etc.). README says `settings.py` is the real source of defaults ("no config.json fallback") (`README.md:20`).

Max Threads label "default: 3" vs yaml 10 / colab 2. `chk_det_size` default hard-coded True regardless of config. `on_option_changed` calls `raise gr.Error` for unmatched controls.

<!-- ===== RU_B ===== -->
## RU slice B — roop-ultimate `face_engine/`, `tools/`, `scripts/`, `benchmarks/`, `portable/`, `web_ui/`, `tests/`, CI/hooks, repo root

Reference: roop-ultimate @71ebb65 (read-only clone). Paths are relative to the repo root. All claims verified by reading the cited file unless tagged [INFERENCE].

### 0. Architecture of this slice

Two code bases live in roop-ultimate. `app/` (slice A) is the shipped Roop Ultimate app (FastAPI + `react-ui/` + `roop.*`). `face_engine/` (this slice) is a **standalone, newer 2D-only accelerator package**: ORT/TensorRT/CUDA runtime, model zoo, GPU detector/tracker/aligner/masker, batched swap/restore, media I/O, a small FastAPI server and its own `web_ui/` (`pyproject.toml:1-38`, `pytest.ini` 3 test trees). It is not imported by `app/` [INFERENCE from pyproject "standalone `face_engine` package only"; not grep-verified for app/].

**There is no 3D volumetric reconstruction anywhere in this slice.** grep for `3drecon|3dmm|fsz|volumetric|flame|deca|mesh` finds only `torch.meshgrid` calls, sidecar-file words, and the `1k3d68` *landmark* model (`face_engine/models/zoo.py:85`, a 68-point 3D-layout landmark net used by the app, FP32-locked `core/trt_compiler.py:269-273`). `.fsz` appears only in `tools/acceptance_stage15.py` (a person_a.fsz faceset file consumed by the app's API; header line 1). Pose is 2D-only: `estimate_yaw_5pt` geometry guess (`core/guardrails.py:90-97`), a profile-guard on Umeyama singular values (`pipeline/aligner.py:727-760`), and LivePortrait keypoints for expression (`processors/expression.py:1-25`). Stereo (`pipeline/stereo.py`) is SBS/TB 2D-per-eye epipolar coupling, not 3D reconstruction.

### 1. face_engine internals (detection / pose / warping / quality / runtime)

#### 1.1 Detection (`pipeline/detector.py`, 1242 lines; `boosted/retinaface_gpu.py`)
- `BaseDetector`: input 640, score 0.5, NMS IoU 0.4 (`detector.py:325-327`); letterbox into a square canvas, `torchvision.ops.batched_nms` with frame index as group. Normalization per model measured on 240 frames: SCRFD `(x-127.5)/128` RGB, YOLOFace `x/255` (`detector.py:1-29`).
- `GPUSCRFDDetector` (tight canvas padded to a multiple of 32, TensorRT FP16): score 0.45, IoU 0.40 (`detector.py:626-627`).
- `AngleResilientSCRFD` (`detector.py:904-1000`): primary pass at current angle; **conditional rot90 sweep** (3 other angles batched at `sweep_size` 640) when no face >= 0.50, or face rolled > `max_pass_roll` 45 deg, or fewer faces than one of the last `lost_window` 2 frames, or tracker asked (`note_track_lost`). Dual thresholds high 0.50 / low 0.20, NMS 0.45, `preferred_hold` 10 frames, scene declared empty after `empty_after` 30 frames then swept every `empty_rescan` 15. This sweep is 90-degree-quantised in-plane roll only; **it is not a yaw/pitch (out-of-plane) detector**. Docstring quantifies SCRFD-at-0deg on a 90deg-rotated t1.jpg: 4/6 faces, 180deg: 2/6, landmarks 5-28% of face size off.
- `RetinaFaceR50Detector` (`boosted/retinaface_gpu.py`): vectorised anchors (`priors`), TensorRT, batched NMS; `strided_tracker` helper. Registered `retinaface_r50` (`models/zoo.py:39`).
- Model zoo (`models/zoo.py`, 461 lines): scrfd_10g_bnkps, retinaface_r50, yoloface_8n (640 fixed, AGPL), hrffa (**declared, `_UNAVAILABLE`**, `zoo.py:60`), 2dfan4 (68-pt, 256 input, 97.9 MB), 2d106det, 1k3d68, arcface_w600k_r50, hyperswap_256/512, alpha_256, alphaface_256 (**urls=() unavailable, `zoo.py:186`**), reswapper_128/256, inswapper_128(+fp16), hififace_256 + crossface_hififace, face_occluder_v3/xseg_3/dfl_xseg_v2/xseg, bisenet_resnet34, sam2_hiera_tiny (.pt), gpen_bfr_512/1024/2048, restoreformer_plus_plus, codeformer_fp16.
- Failure behaviour: guardrails never raise on empty frames (`core/guardrails.py:1-30`); `Face.kps` not clipped so edge-cut faces survive (`detector.py:54`).

#### 1.2 Pose / angle thresholds (`core/guardrails.py`, `pipeline/aligner.py`)
- `estimate_yaw_5pt` (`guardrails.py:90`): geometry-only yaw from nose offset vs half-eye distance, `nose_depth=1.0` half eye distances; saturates when landmarks collapse. No pose network.
- `choose_alignment_points` (`guardrails.py:120-146`): use dense landmarks only if confidence >= `DENSE_MIN_CONFIDENCE` 0.35 and |yaw| <= `DENSE_MAX_YAW_DEG` 75 deg; else 5-point fallback. `MIN_DET` 1e-12, `MIN_SPREAD_PX` 0.5 (`guardrails.py:42-45`); `safe_affine` replaces singular/non-finite matrices.
- `ProfileGuardedAligner` (`aligner.py:807-838`, `profile_guarded_similarity_cuda:727`): closed-form 2x2 Umeyama (no cuSOLVER), floors `s2/s1` at `min_ratio` 0.15, scale only (docstring concedes a similarity cannot stretch; rotation unaffected); padding_mode "reflection". Templates: arcface_112, arcface_128 (+8px x-shift, 256 crops for HyperSwap), ffhq_512 (GPEN/RF++/CodeFormer), arcface_112_v1 (SimSwap 512) (`aligner.py:53-64`). `fit_error` exposed as a gate for profiles (`aligner.py:156`).
- **Wiring gap:** `AngleResilientSCRFD`, `RobustByteTracker`, `ProfileGuardedAligner`, `choose_alignment_points`, `estimate_yaw_5pt`, `EpipolarStereoCoupler`, `HybridVideoMasker`, provenance signer and `UltraRestorer` are referenced only by their own module, `__init__` re-exports and tests (grep over non-test `face_engine/`); `server/processing.py` uses `GPUSCRFDDetector` + `StridedFaceTracker` + `TemporalFaceTracker` only (`processing.py:127,203`). They are library-complete but not on the production render path [INFERENCE: grep shows no caller, dynamic import not excluded].

#### 1.3 Temporal (`pipeline/tracker.py`, 1012 lines)
- `StridedFaceTracker`: full detection every N frames (UI 1-8; presets use 3), pyramidal GPU Lucas-Kanade between (`window` 21, `fb_threshold` 1.0 px, `min_valid_fraction` 0.5, 4 pyramid levels), forced re-detect on cut / lost face / no faces (`tracker.py:1-30,54-72`).
- `SceneCutDetector`: per-channel 32-bin histogram of 64-px-wide frame, 0.5*L1 averaged over channels, `cut_threshold` 0.5 (`tracker.py:70-118`); host read of 1 scalar per frame (`:405`).
- `TemporalFaceTracker`: EMA `alpha` 0.75 (weight on NEW), match cost 0.5*(1-IoU)+0.5*landmark dist, `max_cost` 0.7, `max_missed` 3 retention (`tracker.py:512-534`).
- `RobustByteTracker` (`:816`, Kalman XYAH `:742`): high 0.50 / low 0.20, match IoU high 0.20 (distance form), low 0.50, `max_lost_frames` 5, motion-compensated EMA alpha 0.75; docstring states plain EMA trails motion by `v(1-alpha)/alpha` (`tracker.py:672-714`).
- Quantified claims (synthetic): Stage9 jitter variance 39.72 -> 5.79 px^2 (-85.42%), 24 crossings 100% identity stable, 5-frame dropout recovered (`benchmarks/baselines/benchmark_stage9_temporal_tracking.json`). Stage3 detector 80 frames: every-frame 16.34 fps vs temporal 46.9 fps (2.87x) (`benchmark_stage3_detector.json`).

#### 1.4 Swap / quality (`processors/`, `enhancers/`, `pipeline/masker.py`)
- `swapper.py` (720 lines): `IdentityEncoder` (ArcFace w600k_r50 aligned to arcface_112, 512-d L2-norm), HyperSwap 1a/1b/1c consumes vector directly; inswapper uses `normalize(v @ emap)`. **Pixel Boost** polyphase k*k sub-image swap (256x256/512x512/1024x1024) (`swapper.py:1-40`). Swap sessions built `trt_fp16=False`; FP16 overflow measured: HyperSwap identity distance 0.352->0.407, inswapper "rainbow" smudges (`swapper.py:28-31`). Swappers are 2D image-to-image warps on 256/128 crops: model-bound resolution.
- `masker.py` (637 lines): `M = Feather(Box) * XSeg * Regions * Valid` in the 256 crop (`masker.py:1-5`). XSEG_SIZE 256, PARSER_SIZE 512 (ffhq_512), XSeg threshold 0.1, feather sigma 1.0 px@256, box blur 0.3, BiSeNet-ResNet34 19 CelebAMask-HQ classes; default regions skin, nose, eyes, brows, lips, inner mouth (`masker.py:69-71,102-135`). Notes L/R eye labels unreliable. XSeg output is visible-face probability used un-inverted (`:12-18`).
- `boosted/xseg_masker.py`: morphological erode-then-blur feather on GPU; dual-stream swap || mask (`boosted/ultra_pipeline.py:61-90`); VRAM cap `set_per_process_memory_fraction(0.90)` (`ultra_pipeline.py:~25-30,50`).
- `pipeline/hybrid_masker.py`: SAM2 hiera-tiny memory tracking minus XSeg-3, bbox expanded 15%, dilation 3 px max_pool2d, gaussian sigma 0.5-4.0, 16-frame FIFO (`hybrid_masker.py:1-18`). Tests exist only in `test_modern_stages.py`.
- `processors/color.py`: modes via LAB mean/std transfer; region min 16 px; host (OpenCV 8-bit LAB) and CUDA paths equal within rounding (`color.py:1-25`).
- Restorers: `processors/enhancer.py` GPEN-BFR 512/1024/2048 + RestoreFormer++; `enhancers/ultra_engine.py` Ultra Restore: ScaleAwareEnhancerRouter (bypass / GPEN-512 17.8 ms / GPEN-1024 32.3 ms per face, `scale_router.py:47-52`), LAB lock to the original crop, `STATS_SIZE` 128, `COLLAPSE_STD` 2.0 guard, frequency-split fusion (crop sigma 2.5, 17-px kernel = `2*ceil(3sigma)+1`, `frequency.py:1-30`), BiSeNet region weights (`semantic_fusion.py`). Measured table (`ultra_engine.py:40-50`): linear alpha 0.8 identity med 0.916/p10 0.693 vs split sigma 2.5 0.991/0.959, texture 232% vs 160%, 27.3 vs 32.2 ms/face. GPEN TRT engines require final linear + style pixel-norm pinned FP32 or 1024 NaNs (`ultra_engine.py:3-8`). Engines batch-1 (StyleGAN modulated conv).
- `processors/expression.py`: LivePortrait expression transfer `x_d = x_s + scale*w*(exp_target-exp_s)`, groups lips(6,12,14,17,19,20)/eyes(11,13,15,16,18)/other, blink-sync 97% of frames vs 33% (`expression.py:1-30`); `utils/gridsample5d.py` rewrites 5-D GridSample for TensorRT.
- `pipeline/provenance.py`: offline HMAC-signed manifests in MP4/PNG/WebP + DCT watermark (370 lines); not wired.

#### 1.5 Compute (`core/`)
- `config.py:49`: default provider order TensorRT -> CUDA -> CPU; TRT workspace 4 GiB, `trt_fp16_enable` True, engine cache `.cache/trt_engines` (`config.py:54-64`); `strict` mode turns silent ORT EP drop into `ProviderFallbackError` (`config.py:130-135`, `execution.py`). Env: FACE_ENGINE_DEVICE_ID / CACHE_DIR / MODELS_DIR.
- `core/trt_compiler.py` (710 lines): AOT engine build + verify (NaN reject, fidelity) + json sidecar keyed on TRT version/SM/graph stamp; whole-model FP16 locks xseg, 2d106det, 1k3d68 to FP32 (`trt_compiler.py:269-273`). `zero_copy_engine.py`: ORT IOBinding/OrtValue with profile min 256 / max 512 (`:1-20`; hard-coded `get_default_face_shape_profile`). `utils/onnx_batch.py` dynamic batch + FP16 + InstanceNorm decomposition. `cuda_streams.py` (579 lines): triple-buffered decode/inference/encode on three streams, event hand-off, ring buffer pinned+VRAM; NVDEC "loses once inference shares the GPU and deadlocks in-process" so software PyAV decode is the shipped path (`cuda_streams.py:8-13`).
- Measured presets (`server/processing.py:90-96`): RTX 4070, 1080p, 2 faces, 600 frames, spec targets 60/30/12 fps vs measured 56.2/44.5/8.7 (Stage 4-6 sequential 50.3/40.4/8.6).

#### 1.6 Media / jobs (`media/`, `server/`)
- Output: libx264 `-preset medium -crf 18 -pix_fmt yuv420p +faststart`, BT.709/BT.2020 tagging; ffmpeg 8.1 colour-tag workaround (`ffmpeg_pipe.py:9-14,64-68,199-236`); NVENC writer with x264 fallback (`encoder.py`); lossless demux to `.m4a`/`.mka` sidecars (not raw .aac, 21.3 ms late) (`demuxer.py:17`); A/V sync check tolerance one frame period (`guardrails.py:169-206`).
- `worker_pool.py`: GOP-aligned segments, one spawned process per chunk, seamless concat; `ipc_pool.py` shared-memory ring (613 lines).
- `server/state.py`: **one in-memory Job**, states preparing/rendering/completed/failed/cancelled, `threading.Event` cancel, job thread (`state.py:113-126,366-406`). No persisted queue, no checkpoint/resume in face_engine (the app has pause/resume via root `pause.js`/`resume.js`).
- REST (`server/api.py`): `/api/project/load`, `/api/project`, `/api/project/assign`, `/api/detect/faces`, `/api/options`, `/api/pipeline/start|stop|status`, `/api/outputs/{filename}`, `/api/media/target`, `/api/thumbnails/{name}`; preview routes in `preview.py`; `/ws/telemetry` in `telemetry.py` (NVML else nvidia-smi sampler). `RenderParams` is `extra="forbid"`: detector (scrfd/retinaface/yolo), swapper, pixel_boost, mask_types box/occlusion/region, enhancer_blend 0-100, provider, mask padding, mask_blur 0.3, match_threshold 0.3, detection_stride 1-8, temporal_smoothing, workers 1-8 (`processing.py:59-83`).

### 2. Precision and CUDA policy documents

- `PRECISION_POLICY.md` (226 lines): model-specific precision matrix (safe/candidate/unsafe/not-validated/unsupported). Facts: GPEN-1024/2048 and GFPGAN **FP32-only**; all swap nets FP16 `unsafe`, mixed `candidate`; XSeg mixed `unsafe` (IoU min 0.81); landmarks 2d106det/1k3d68 FP32 override (drift 1.2-8.8 px); RestoreFormer++ FP32 default (21.63 -> 39.31 ms/face, +82%). Swap INT8 on hyperswap_1a (RTX 4070, TRT 10.9.0.34, 186 faces): INT8 3.69 ms but identity -0.026 (59.1% of faces lose >0.02) => unsafe; FP8 unsupported on Ada (0 layers run FP8, 14.48 ms). RTX 3060 results **pending**. Enforced via `precision_policy.PRECISION_OVERRIDES` (app side, slice A).
- `CUDA_EXECUTION_POLICY.md` (174 lines): stream policy by VRAM (<7 GB => 1 stream, 0 TRT aux; >=7 GB => <=2 streams, <=1 aux); CUDA-graph GPEN-256 Pro filter rejected (23% slower); fork-join XSeg on second stream +15.9% single pair but +2.2-3.0% (noise) with 2-4 frame workers; `cv2->grid_sample` and `dilate->max_pool2d` rejected (ellipse vs square error up to 1.0); end-to-end 7.74 fps / 13.58 fps render phase.
- `PERFORMANCE_BASELINE.md` (261 lines): locked 2026-08-29 baseline, i9-14900K / RTX 4070 12 GB / 32 GB; final Stage-15: 7.74 fps e2e 600 frames, VRAM peak 12,073 MB, decode 21.3 / detect 18.7 / swap 97.0 / mask 35.2 / enhance 99.8 / blend 37.1 ms per call; software decode 204 fps, hevc_nvenc 880 fps. Explicit not-run list (benchmark_pipeline k1.mp4, RTX 3060).
- `OPTIMIZATION_PLAN.md` (717 lines): 16-phase plan + gates A-E (adversarial review, targets, Rubin, i9 CPU, unified scheduler). Current gate: physical RTX 3060 Laptop validation of phases 0-4 pending.

### 3. Benchmarks (`benchmarks/baselines`, `scripts/benchmarks`, `tools/benchmark_*`, `tests/benchmark_pipeline.py`)

Stage0 baseline (RTX 4070, 13 scenarios, `benchmark_stage0_summary.csv`; **synthetic fixtures**, [INFERENCE] from `tests/benchmark_pipeline.py` SyntheticFace/`generate_synthetic_video_fixture`): frontal 11.62 fps / ID 0.9854; 45-degree 11.62 / 0.9069; **extreme profile 4.36 fps, 25 detection failures, ID 0.467**; small face ID 0.767; multi-interacting faces 0.33 fps ID 0.4238; partial occlusion ID 0.8027; high-motion landmark instability 0.8553; 1080p 0.50 fps; 4K 0.64 fps; VRAM saturates at ~12.1 GB from scenario 7 on. Stage files 2-9 cover lifecycle, detector, alignment, HyperSwap audit v2 (quality via `tools/quality_harness.py` after earlier typed-in values were declared INVALID), restore-ultra profiles FAST/BALANCED/QUALITY/ULTRA, xseg3, compositing, temporal. `trace_frame_analysis.json`: 1280x720 single frame 286.3 ms.

### 4. Per-file inventory

Status legend used in purpose text: files under `face_engine/` are real implementations unless noted in section 6; `tools/apply_*.py` are one-shot source patchers (see 6); `tools/test_*.py`, `tests/*` are tests/probes. Bytes = inventory bytes; lines = `wc -l`.

### 5. Test and tool group notes (what is covered)

- **`tests/` (56 files, root integration suite)**: Stage-numbered suites 2-27 (`test_stage*`), benchmark harness tests, TRT engine build/precision-surgeon/quant tests (`test_build_trt_engines` 19 tests, `test_prebuild_engines` 18, `test_trt_quant`, `test_trt_precision_surgeon`), fused post-process kernel (`test_fused_postprocess` 23 + fallback 10), RetinaFace closeup (`test_retinaface_closeup`), angle tests (`test_angles` 11: omnidirectional rectification + profile-aware weighted Umeyama on the app side), occlusion interaction, stereo epipolar (`test_stereo_epipolar_disparity`), GPU bindings (10). Marked GPU tests skipped in CI (`ci.yml:100-101` `-m "not gpu"`).
- **`face_engine/tests/` (27 files)**: real GPU tests (`test_pipeline` 498 lines, `test_stage3_gpu`, `test_cuda_streams`, `test_video_hw`, `test_trt_compiler`) plus `bench_*`/`eval_*`/`verify_*` scripts and `test_web_ui_e2e` (headless Chromium against real server). `test_modern_stages.py` (4 tests) covers the "modernised" files `core/base_processor.py`, `core/zero_copy_engine.py`, `processors/modern_processors.py`, `pipeline/hybrid_masker.py` (thin).
- **`tools/` (69 top-level files)**: (a) benchmark harnesses per stage (`benchmark_*`, `bench_*`, `quality_harness.py` 1138 lines: SSIM/PSNR/mask-IoU/ArcFace cosine vs FP32 reference); (b) TRT build/convert (`build_trt_engines`, `compile_engines`, `prebuild_engines`, `convert_fp16`, `convert_dynamic_shapes`, `restorer_native_engine`, `diagnose_trt`); (c) landmark/eye/mouth index probes (`probe_*`, `derive_eye_ear_order`, `verify_eye_ear_symmetry`); (d) cut-detector probes (`cut_threshold_probe`, `scene_cut_audit`, `verify_cut_detector`, `ab_tracking_prepass`); (e) acceptance runs through live API (`acceptance_stage15`, `acceptance_real_clip`); (f) telemetry/terminal (`terminal_monitor.py` 353 lines, `live_monitor`, `telemetry_sidecar`, `probe_telemetry_latency`, `analyze_telemetry`); (g) generators (`gen_settings`, `gen_update_manifest`, `gen_jitter_fixture`, `gen_hud_fixture`, `fit_identity_directions`); (h) one-shot patchers `apply_*.py` (5 files, string-replace source edits).
- **`scripts/`**: Pinokio `clean.js`/`fix_tensorrt.js`, `cleanup.py` (disk cleanup of TRT caches/old logs/uploads), `profile_pipeline.py`, `verify_roop_keep.py` (corpus verifier), `verification/check_api_mock_parity.py` (CI contract check), `verify_pipeline.py`, `verify_integration.py` (Stage 19).
- **Root launchers** (`pinokio.js` 195 lines; `install.js`, `start.js`->`start_react.js`, `start_legacy.js` (DEPRECATED Gradio), `update.js`, `reset.js`, `pause.js`/`resume.js`/`stop.js` POST `/api/pause|resume|stop`, `torch.js`): install gated on markers `.pinokio-install-complete.json`/`.pinokio-install-ready.json` and `react-ui/dist/index.html` (`pinokio.js:9-16`); installs via `uv pip`, `npm ci`, SAM2 `--no-deps`, `onnxconverter-common==1.16.0`, `numpy==1.26.4` pin (`install.js:119-146`). `run.py` forwards to `app/run.py` and sets `PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:False,max_split_size_mb:128` (`run.py:3`). `pinokio_batch_runner.py`: one fresh GPU process per video over retained folders, with queue progress (`:1-30`). `portable/bootstrap.py`: stdlib-only zero-install runtime (uv + CPython 3.10, torch CUDA 12.8, TRT 10.9, static FFmpeg, offline `--build-bundle`).
- **CI** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`): react-ui lint+build+offline-bundle check, root typecheck, python light-profile tests on matrix OS, `gen_settings --check`, `check_api_mock_parity`, `gen_update_manifest --check`. **.githooks/pre-commit** regenerates `update_manifest.json` and refuses root scratch files/logs.
- **Agent files** (`AGENTS.md` 767 lines, CLAUDE/GEMINI/QWEN.md, .clinerules/.cursorrules/.windsurfrules): treated as data; not followed.

### Axis findings (slice)

1. **Detection/landmarks/angle + failure thresholds** — SCRFD 0.45/0.40, AngleResilient 0.50/0.20/0.45 with 45-deg roll trigger and 3-angle rot90 sweep (`detector.py:626,952-960`); dense-landmark gate conf 0.35 and |yaw| 75 deg, 5-pt fallback (`guardrails.py:47-48,138-143`); geometry-only yaw (`guardrails.py:90`); profile guard min_ratio 0.15 (`aligner.py:807`). Measured failure: extreme-profile scenario 25/30 detection failures, ID 0.467 at 4.36 fps (`benchmark_stage0_summary.csv` row 3). No out-of-plane pose network (hrffa unavailable `zoo.py:60`).
2. **2D warping vs 3D** — purely 2D: 5-pt similarity to 112/128→256 crops, Pixel Boost polyphase (`swapper.py:1-40`), kornia `warp_affine` reflection padding. No mesh/3DMM/volumetric/.fsz in face_engine; `1k3d68` is only a landmark model; LivePortrait expression keypoints are the only 3D-ish latent (`expression.py`). Stereo is per-eye 2D coupling (`stereo.py:1-18`, delta_y < 0.05 px, EMA disparity).
3. **Visual quality** — mask = box x XSeg(0.1 thr) x BiSeNet (`masker.py:1-5,102`); LAB colour transfer (`color.py`); Ultra Restore frequency split sigma 2.5, identity 0.916→0.991 vs linear blend (`ultra_engine.py:40-50`); GPEN-512/1024 (1024 and 2048 FP32-only per policy); swap model resolution fixed 128/256 (Pixel Boost up to 1024 via polyphase). Compositing benchmark metrics are synthetic.
4. **Temporal** — LK flow stride + histogram cut detector 0.5 (`tracker.py:70`), EMA 0.75, 3-frame retention, ByteTrack 5-frame lost (`tracker.py:512-714`); synthetic -85.42% jitter. High-motion scenario instability 0.8553, entering/leaving 1.1823 in Stage0.
5. **CLI/terminal/telemetry/diagnostics** — `tools/terminal_monitor.py` ANSI dashboard, `live_monitor.py`, `telemetry_sidecar.py`, `analyze_telemetry.py`; `/ws/telemetry` NVML/nvidia-smi sampler (`server/telemetry.py`); `face_engine/benchmark.py` (per-stage latency, VRAM, sync locks); `tools/audit_system.py` (1124 lines production audit); `tools/diagnose_trt.py`; `tools/trace_pipeline_frame.py` flamegraph.
6. **Compute** — TRT→CUDA→CPU default with strict-fail option (`config.py:49,130`); AOT engines with sidecars; FP32 locks for swap nets, XSeg, landmarks, GPEN-1024+ (`PRECISION_POLICY.md`, `trt_compiler.py:269`); INT8/FP8 rejected on Ada; 90% torch VRAM cap (`ultra_pipeline.py`); VRAM saturates ~12.1 GB in Stage0 (4070); RTX 3060 validation pending everywhere; NVDEC disabled in-process (`cuda_streams.py:8`).
7. **Job queue/checkpoint/video IO/FFmpeg** — single job, thread + cancel event (`state.py:113-126`); GOP-segment worker pool spawn (`worker_pool.py`); libx264 crf18 +faststart; NVENC; lossless demux/remux; A/V sync check; no persisted queue/checkpoint in face_engine; batch runner `pinokio_batch_runner.py` (fresh process per video); pause/resume only via app API.
8. **Workstation UI** — `web_ui/` React 18 + Vite 6 + Tailwind 4: project loader, face grid, parameter sliders, DualCanvasPlayer, live preview hook, telemetry HUD (24 files; `web_ui/README.md`). Separate from app `react-ui/` (slice other). Pinokio menu launcher `pinokio.js` + `portable/`.

### Gaps / stubs / defects found

- **No 3D volumetric / faceset path in this repo slice**; the reference is a 2D swapper stack; `.fsz` is only consumed by the app API in `tools/acceptance_stage15.py`.
- **Library-only modules, no production caller** in non-test face_engine: `AngleResilientSCRFD`, `RobustByteTracker`, `ProfileGuardedAligner`, `choose_alignment_points`/`estimate_yaw_5pt`, `EpipolarStereoCoupler`/stereo, `HybridVideoMasker`, `provenance`, `UltraRestorer`, `zero_copy_engine`, `modern_processors` (grep evidence, section 1.2). The server renders with `GPUSCRFDDetector` + Strided/Temporal trackers.
- Model zoo gaps: `hrffa` and `alphaface_256` registered unavailable (`zoo.py:60,186`), yet `web_ui/src/lib/types.ts` and `RenderParams` still expose `alphaface_256` as a selectable swapper.
- Yaw estimator is geometry-only and saturates on collapsed landmarks (`guardrails.py:97`); profile guard changes scale only (`aligner.py:727-760` docstring).
- SCRFD ByteTrack `match_iou_high` default 0.20 vs ByteTrack's 0.8-as-distance reference: documented, not tested against real occlusion/crossing footage (Stage9 crossing results synthetic, naive == robust 100%).
- Benchmarks: Stage 3/4/8/9 baselines are synthetic; Stage0 scenarios 8-13 run at 0.33-1.2 fps (Multiple Faces Interacting 0.33 fps, ID 0.4238) — quality/perf floor for hard cases; Stage 5 file documented as previously INVALID (typed-in quality values).
- Baseline pending: RTX 3060 validation (OPTIMIZATION_PLAN.md CURRENT GATE, PRECISION_POLICY INT8 tier, CUDA policy), `benchmark_pipeline.py` default clip missing (`PERFORMANCE_BASELINE.md` not-run list).
- `tools/apply_*.py` (5) are one-shot string-replace patchers of other source files (`apply_browser_video_updates.py` 723 lines, etc.) kept in tree: drift/hazard if re-run.
- Single in-memory job in `face_engine/server/state.py`: no queue, no checkpoint/resume, no persistence across restart.
- Hard-coded TRT profile in `zero_copy_engine.py` (min 256 / max 512 shapes, `:1-20`) — baseline-only vs the AOT `trt_compiler` path.
- 4K scenario throughput 0.64 fps and 1080p 0.5 fps in Stage0 vs 56 fps claim in presets: different config (Stage0 full-quality app pipeline vs face_engine TRT presets); not directly comparable [INFERENCE].
- Three agent-rule stub files and AGENTS.md at root carry directives; ignored per audit rules.

### Coverage

Slice files in inventory: 309 | files accounted for: 309 | omitted: none
(verified: `awk` over `roop_ultimate_files.tsv` for prefixes face_engine/ 88, tools/ 69, tests/ 56, web_ui/ 24, benchmarks/ 12, scripts/ 10, portable/ 6, .github/ 1, .githooks/ 1, root 42; table rows = 309.)


<!-- ===== RU_C ===== -->
## RU_C — roop-ultimate slice C: react-ui/, docs/, app/tests/

Reference: roop-ultimate @71ebb65 (read-only clone). Method: every file in the slice was opened programmatically; per-file purposes below are taken from each file's own header comment/docstring (or first test names), not guessed. Items marked [INFERENCE] are not directly verified.

### 0. Executive summary

- **UI = React 19-style SPA (Vite) with 9 hash routes** (`react-ui/src/App.jsx:98-107`: home, faceswap, batch, processing[transient], facemgr, extras, gallery, history, settings). Face Swap + Processing are eager; others lazy (`App.jsx:37-75`).
- **There is NO 3D volumetric viewport / mesh / point-cloud view.** The only "3D" UI items are (a) a `use_3d_recon` toggle that merely requests a pose-matched source crop for image-source swappers (`FaceSwap.jsx:4007`, `defaults.js:103-105` default false), (b) a WebGL2 equirectangular VR180/360 + stereo inspector (`StereoVRViewport.jsx:15-16,29,180`), (c) 3D `.cube` LUT grading (`cubeLut.js`, `webgl2GradingPipeline.js`). Face geometry is 2D warp + similarity transform (`trajectory_cache`, M/M_inv + yaw/pitch/roll).
- Present: split/diagonal wipe compare (`CompareSlider.jsx:20` angle 0..360), multi-track NLE timeline with minimap/ruler/scene-cut/presence/identity-keyframe/override lanes (`timeline/*`), head-pose HUD yaw/pitch/roll (`headPoseModel.js:10` BAR_RANGE yaw 90/pitch 60/roll 45), pose radar for face-bank coverage (`facemgr/PoseRadar.jsx`), mask brush (`maskBrush.js:50`) + Bezier roto layer (`RotoLayer.jsx`, feather 0-120 px at :321), ParserRegions (19 CelebAMask-HQ classes), terminal-mirror dashboard, TRT compile banner, pipeline pill, flamegraph.
- **docs/** (328 files): 123 markdown + 205 measured artefacts (JSON/TXT/logs, TRT engine reports) under `docs/perf/`. Several `docs/audits/STAGE*` reports are explicitly flagged *synthetic inputs / INVALID - DO NOT CITE* (marked 2026-10-09) — treat their numbers as non-evidence.
- **app/tests/** (717 files; 6453 `def test_` by regex count): 474 `test_*.py` pytest modules + 243 harness/probe/diag/support files (A/B scripts, probes, fixtures, one `.npz`).

##### 1.3 What the UI test layer certifies (react-ui/.render-check + e2e)
- Logic checks run in plain Node/Vite SSR: batch matrix staging (`batch-matrix-check.mjs`), brush→canvas mapping sub-pixel (`brush-mapping-check.mjs`), face mapping/roles, retry policy (3 retries, 250/500/1000 ms), frame protocol + player pipeline (`player-check.mjs`), head-pose HUD model (`render-head-pose-hud.mjs`: signed 1-decimal angles, dash for missing), history/undo engine + A/B/C/D slots (`render-history.mjs`), identity keyframes (stage 20), LUT/scopes (stage 18), BroadcastChannel monitor (stage 19), stereo VR (stage 21), NLE EDL/OTIO + VFX matte + provenance (stage 22), parameter curves + context menu (stage 25), expression parser + shortcuts (stage 26), resizable workspace store defaults leftWidth 350/rightWidth 330/bottomHeight 280 (stage 27), timeline logic, pipeline pill/report/controls, TRT compile banner (stage 14), console mirror from a REAL backend-generated snapshot, mutation-check (re-introduces bugs, asserts red).
- Playwright e2e (production build + mock server): axe a11y with allowlist ratchet, WCAG contrast per theme, frame-budget/long-task, idle CPU/request budget (≤6 stage-frame requests / 30 s when preview 500s), nav visibility, tab-stop count, offline (no external requests), frame-busy 503, frame-unavailable retry, head-pose HUD, timeline, stage5/11/25/26 etc.

### 2. docs/ (328 files)

#### 2.1 Markdown docs — one line each (key claim)

- `BENCHMARKS.md` — **Benchmarks and validation**: How performance and quality are measured on the two validation targets (RTX 4070 12 GB desktop and RTX 3060 Laptop 6 GB). Moved here from the README on 2026-09-22; the text is unchanged. The maintained result tables are
- `CHANGELOG.md` — **Changelog and incident notes**: Dated notes on behaviour changes and the incidents behind them, in reverse order. The full session record is [`SESSION_LOGS.md`](SESSION_LOGS.md); the running engineering state lives outside the repository (`RECODE_STATUS.md` in the operator's `roop-keep`
- `CPU_GATE_D.md` — **Gate D — Intel hybrid CPU optimization**: Gate D adds capability-driven CPU profiling and controlled CPU-distribution selection. It does not make i9-14900K behavior mandatory and does not claim a CPU policy is globally optimal without end-to-end evidence on both required
- `DETECTION_INTERACTION_TRACEABILITY_2026-09-23.md` — **Detection and interacting-face traceability matrix**: Date: 2026-09-23 This matrix maps every explicit scenario in the request and every changed public behavior to a concrete check and its observed result. It deliberately separates detector evidence from proof that a selected source was actually applied in a complete video render. - Exact required gate
- `ENV_FLAGS.md` — **Environment Flags (`ROOP_*`)**: All runtime tunables read from environment variables. Every flag is **opt-in or safe-by-default** — with none set, the app runs its validated default behavior. Values are read at process start (some at module import), so set them **before**
- `FACESET_V2.md` — **FaceSet V2 format**: `.fsz` V2 = ZIP of legacy PNGs + deterministic `metadata.json` (schema roop.fsz v2, sorted keys, fixed ZIP timestamps, SHA-256 per member); per-source identity embedding, bbox, 68-pt + 3D 68 landmarks, yaw/pitch/roll, quality, appearance, expression, detail masks. Invalid CRC/unsafe names rejected.
- `FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md` — **Roop Ultimate — final optimization architecture**: Phase 16 flow: decode→detect/quality→tracks+identity+pose+expression→FaceSet lookup+pose-aware source→align→illumination adapt→identity-detail restore→enhancer→mask→temporal compositing→temporal QC→encode/mux; TRT cache namespaced by precision/SM/CUDA/driver/TRT/ORT; CPU always last-resort provider.
- `FINAL_VALIDATION_MATRIX.md` — **Final validation matrix**: Master record for the final end-to-end validation, repair and regression campaign. One row per test. A row is PASS only when a run on this repository's current code produced the evidence named in its own cell.
- `GATE_ABE_4070.md` — **Gates A, B and E — RTX 4070, 2026-08-30**: All measurements are end-to-end renders of the locked controlled fixture (`d4.mp4`, two facesets, realswap / GPEN 256 Pro / RealityUX, TensorRT mixed) at **600 frames — production length**. Short-window (120-frame) numbers appear
- `GATE_A_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW.md` — **Gate A — Independent Adversarial Performance Review**: Date: 2026-08-29 Scope: current repository implementation and performance harnesses, before the Gate A fixes in this review.
- `HARDWARE_VALIDATION.md` — **Hardware Validation Ledger**: This file is the per-change validation ledger required by the dual-GPU policy. The RTX 3060 and RTX 4070 are never combined into one generic benchmark. The detailed historical matrix is in [`HARDWARE_VALIDATION_MATRIX.md`](HARDWARE_VALIDATION_MATRIX.md).
- `HARDWARE_VALIDATION_MATRIX.md` — **Dual-GPU hardware validation matrix**: This is the acceptance record for the two first-class NVIDIA targets. Results are never averaged across GPUs. Runtime identity is detected from the active stack and persisted with `hardware_profile_key`; free VRAM is telemetry and is
- `HDR_PIPELINE.md` — **HDR / high-bit-depth colour path**: `app/roop/hdr_color.py` holds the colour maths and `app/roop/hdr_pipeline.py` the I/O. Settings live in the **Output** section: `hdr_pipeline`, `hdr_source_transfer`, `hdr_source_primaries` and `hdr_output_codec` (env `ROOP_HDR`, `ROOP_HDR_TRANSFER`,
- `LIVE_MODE.md` — **Real-Time / Live Webcam Mode**: Live webcam: buffer_size=1 latest-frame mailbox, detector every **6th** frame + Lucas-Kanade flow between, histogram scene-cut detector flushes temporal state, **45 ms** target budget (unverified), enhancers disabled, OBS virtual cam via pyvirtualcam, endpoints `/api/livecam/*`.
- `OPTIMIZATION_PROGRESS.md` — **Optimization Progress**: Full record: `docs/FINAL_VALIDATION_MATRIX.md`, section `# RTX 4070 campaign (2026-09-01)`. Handoff and next steps: `docs/PHASE_HANDOFF.md`, top section.
- `PERFORMANCE_OPTIMIZATION_HANDOFF.md` — **Performance Optimization Handoff**: **PERFORMANCE FOUNDATION COMPLETE — READY FOR REALISM/QUALITY OPTIMIZATION** This document is the authoritative handoff for the performance-foundation work completed through the stable implementation point below. It describes what is
- `PHASE11_ENHANCER_INVENTORY.md` — **Phase 11 enhancer inventory**: Status: source-tree inventory completed 2026-09-01. This document is the authoritative inventory for Phase 11; it is based on the implementations under `app/roop/processors`, the registered processors in `app/roop/ProcessMgr.py`,
- `PHASE11_ENHANCER_MATRIX.md` — **Phase 11 enhancer performance matrix**: This is the complete matrix schema for the 30 source-discovered enhancement, restoration, colorization, and super-resolution paths. The benchmark stores one copy per hardware profile; rows are never averaged across GPUs. `pending`
- `PHASE6_POSE_QUALITY.md` — **Phase 6 Pose and Source-Bank Quality Evaluation**: Pose eval harness; selector match tolerance **15° yaw & pitch**; V1 `.fsz` archives had five real yaw plates incl. profile, no pitch/inversion plates (coverage gap flagged).
- `PHASE6_TEMPORAL_IDENTITY.md` — **Phase 6 Temporal Identity Consistency Engine**: `TemporalIdentityStabilizer` (opt-in `ROOP_TEMPORAL_IDENTITY=1`): source-bank switch after **3** persistent requests, bounded immediate re-estimation on major yaw/pitch/roll change; low-frequency-only output blending; crop blending bypassed on parallel stab path.
- `PHASE7_OCCLUSION.md` — **Phase 7 - Temporal Occlusion and Interacting-Face Engine**: Temporal occlusion engine (opt-in `ROOP_TEMPORAL_OCCLUSION`): event threshold **0.12**, interaction **0.08**, stable frames 3, refresh 5, appearance 0.16, leave alpha 0.35, enter alpha 0.90; mask-only transitions.
- `PHASE8_EXPRESSION.md` — **Phase 8 — Target Expression Preservation**: Model-free expression layer on 106-pt landmarks (opt-in): alpha 0.28, motion alpha 0.82, closed ratio 0.48, open ratio 0.70, event strength 0.86; independent per-eye blink/wink states; real-video validation still OPEN.
- `PHASE_HANDOFF.md` — **Handoff - shape-profile A/B: CLOSED, no measurable effect (2026-09-03, final)**: Device: RTX 4070, driver 616.56, TRT 10.9.0.34, ORT 1.23.2, i9-14900K, 31.7 GB. Commits `d542608`, `c264e86`, `4ec1f0f`, `66d754e`, `91c9d48`. Suite **2002 green**, 1 skipped. Supersedes the VOID run described below; the guard that
- `POSE_AWARE_SOURCE_SELECTION.md` — **Phase 5: Pose-aware source selection**: Pose record `_pose_v5` (yaw/pitch/roll/scale/off-axis/perspective risk); source scoring on yaw/pitch/circular-roll + expression + lighting; switch hysteresis margin default **0.035** (`ROOP_POSE_SOURCE_SWITCH_MARGIN`); requests 3D crop correction when pose missing, between sparse bank poses, inverted, high perspective risk; 3D plan bounds yaw shear, tighter pitch shear.
- `SECOND_GPU_VALIDATION.md` — **Validating the second GPU**: Everything here is capability-detected at runtime. Nothing needs editing to move between cards: models, thread count, pool sizes and the safety policy all come from the machine's own `config.yaml` and its own detected VRAM tier.
- `SESSION_LOGS.md` — **Session Log Archive — Roop Ultimate**: Verbatim session logs moved out of `CLAUDE.md` on 2026-09-14 to keep the always-loaded file small. Nothing here was edited. #### 3.2 Behaviours certified (sampled from test names; evidence = test files)

- **Angle / pose**: `test_pose_source_selector.py` (15): prefers matching lateral pose over frontal; yaw 30° between bank 0/60 → conservative 3D bridge; yaw 58 w/o profile source → 3D; inverted orientation never hidden by good yaw match; hysteresis stops near-tie flips; yaw grid frontal→profile w/o frontal bias. `test_pose_canonicalization.py`: roll 60° uprighted with exact inverse; profile anchors cannot displace model template. `test_head_pose.py` (30): canonical (yaw,pitch,roll) order never guessed from magnitudes; EPnP poses rank profile as least frontal. `test_angle_portfolio.py` (14): lattice bins follow physical head direction, nearest-neighbour fill, pitch relative to person's neutral (near-frontal only), fused embedding frontal-only. `test_gpu_filmstrip_and_scene_cuts.py`: bitstream cut detector spikes + chapter markers, sparse pre-scan HW path, trim-range keyframe mid-points. `test_stage8_border_and_roll_geometry.py`, `test_visibility_alignment.py`, `test_face_frontalize_orientation.py` cover alignment under roll/border.
- **Temporal**: `test_temporal_smoother.py` (37): 1-euro beta continuous across threshold, monotonic in velocity, velocity normalised by face scale, frame gap declines instead of blending, per-track state isolated. `test_flow_coast.py` (34): similarity fit recovers translation to sub-pixel, rotation+scale, chains over 10-frame dropout, refuses jumps beyond LK range, featureless/two-equal-motions not believed. `test_trajectory_cache.py` (34): known similarity recovered exactly, non-square pixels fitted in display space, unusable rows NaN, flags say why a face is not a detection, pose in head_pose convention. `test_face_tracker.py`: crossing faces keep track ids. Also tracks: assignment, crossshot, gapfill, reid, stitch, coasting; temporal identity/occlusion/expression/quality/color/compositing/smart_skip/parallel_blocks.
- **Queue / lifecycle**: `test_queue.py` (33): jobs survive restart, interrupted job → runnable again, restart does not auto-resume batch, stale reorder cannot drop a job, running job cannot be edited/removed, clear keeps the mid-render job, target resolved by name not stored index, missing target fails that job only. Plus `test_queue_segments.py`, `test_project_checkpoint.py`, `test_batch_matrix_queue.py`, `test_progress_chunks.py`.
- **Compute**: provider fallback is loud, provider discovery safe, TRT build guard/override/context manager, per-model precision, canary cosine floors, FP16 islands, VRAM/OOM guards, small-card admission, zero-copy ring, CUDA graph/gridsample5d.
- **Video IO**: ffmpeg writer colour/lifecycle, NVENC/QSV encode, range seek, NVDEC reader, finalize web video, early close, frame cache/ws.

### 4. Axis findings (slice)

1. **Detection/landmarks/angle/failure thresholds** — UI shows pose bounds only as HUD scale (yaw ±90, pitch ±60, roll ±45: `headPoseModel.js:10`); no UI-side failure threshold. Docs: selector tolerance 15° yaw/pitch (`docs/PHASE6_POSE_QUALITY.md`), switch hysteresis 0.035 (`POSE_AWARE_SOURCE_SELECTION.md`), Stage4 audit (synthetic) cites yaw>35° far-eye collapse, >45° 3-point solver. Tests pin canonicalization at roll 60° (`test_pose_canonicalization.py:39`). Face bank pose coverage UI: `facemgr/PoseRadar.jsx`.
2. **2D warping vs 3D/faceset/.fsz** — Entire reference is 2D similarity warp (trajectory cache M/M_inv, `perf/phase14/README.md`). "3D" = optional pose-matched source crop (`use_3d_recon`, off by default, no effect on inswapper-family: `FaceSwap.jsx:4007`). `.fsz` V2 (`FACESET_V2.md`) stores 3D-68 landmarks + yaw/pitch/roll per source; tests `test_faceset_v2.py`, `test_gpu_filmstrip_and_scene_cuts.py::test_fsz_v2_pose_matched_embedding_and_facemgr`. No volumetric UI.
3. **Visual quality/blending/masks/colour/restorers** — Restorer pipeline stack UI (`EnhancerPipelineStack.jsx`: Swapper → Restorer → Texture&Grain → LAB colour), swapper presets inswapper_128 / ReSwapper 256 / Alpha 256 / HyperSwap 256 / HyperSwap 512 (`pipelineRules.js:SWAPPER_PRESETS`), restorer auto-bypass for alpha/hyperswap (`pipelineRules.js` BYPASS_SWAPPERS), 4/9-up comparison grids, parser regions (19 classes), LUT/scopes. Docs: matte ROI, grain match, fused Triton post-process, FP16 islands, PRECISION policies (`docs/perf/*`).
4. **Temporal tracking/smoothing/scene-cut** — UI four modes (`temporalModes.js:28`), cut-aware is engine property not a setting; docs Phase 6/7/8 opt-ins with listed thresholds; Phase14 K-interval verdict (default 1) and one_euro option (default adaptive); live mode histogram cut detector. Tests: smoother/flow/trajectory suites above.
5. **CLI/terminal/telemetry/diagnostics** — Terminal dashboard mirrored in UI from real `roop/terminal_dashboard.py` snapshot (`ConsoleMirror.jsx`, fixture generated by backend), HUD pipeline pill + report, TelemetryPill (GPU/VRAM/CPU), flamegraph stage bars (decode/detect/swap/mask/restore), failure log (`failureLog.js`), `docs/development/TERMINAL_CONTRACT.md`.
6. **Compute** — UI: provider benchmark/matrix (`ProviderBenchmark.jsx`, `AutoTunePanel.jsx` screens provider × swap batch × NVENC), TRT cache panel, compile banner. Docs: dual-GPU matrix (RTX 4070 12GB / 3060 Laptop 6GB, never averaged), TRT 10.9.0.34 / ORT 1.23.2, CUDA_EXECUTION_POLICY, aux FP16 NOT shipped (stays FP32), cross-frame swap batching 4-9% slower on 2-person footage.
7. **Job queue/checkpoint/video IO** — UI jobStore reattach, ResumableCheckpoints, SegmentMatrix (queued/rendering/completed/failed; unknown → queued), Batch Matrix; docs JOB_CONTRACT, STAGE12 video IO, NVENC quality options, QSV mixed-encoder concat failure notes; tests listed in §3.
8. **Workstation UI** — Rail + Header + LayoutSwitcher presets (Standard, Max Canvas, Inspection Suite, My Roto Setup, Dual-Monitor Color, Minimal Laptop: `LayoutSwitcher.jsx`), resizable panels (350/330/280 defaults), undo/redo with 50-action popover (`HistoryPopover.jsx`, `historyEngine.js`), rebindable shortcuts (`shortcuts/actions.js`), command palette, PiP mini-viewport, pop-out reference monitor (`#/monitor`, BroadcastChannel `roop_ultimate_reference_out`), 38 themes + Theme Studio, offline-bundle verification.

### 5. Gaps / stubs / defects found

- **No 3D/volumetric viewer**: nothing in react-ui renders mesh/volume/depth; `use_3d_recon` documented as NO-OP for the inswapper/ghost/hyperswap/simswap family (`FaceSwap.jsx:4007`).
- **UI 2.0 was removed (2026-09-02)** but 11 `docs/development/UI2_*` docs remain as SUPERSEDED; ENVIRONMENT_CONTRACT.md still says "React UI 2.0 is the default client" while CURRENT_STATE/SESSION_HANDOFF say UI 1.0 is sole client — **doc drift**.
- **Invalid/synthetic audit reports**: `docs/audits/STAGE3-STAGE9_*` carry synthetic-input banners; STAGE5 is INVALID - DO NOT CITE. Do not import their numbers.
- **Flow/neural trade-off**: K>1 pre-pass interval loses source-bound frames (K=8: -31% on Love) → opt-in only (`phase14/README.md`).
- **Aux batching, FP16 aux models, GPU ROI paste, temporal colour EMA, cross-frame swap batching**: all measured and NOT shipped/wired (`perf/aux_batch_2026-10-05.md`, `aux_fp16_onnx_2026-10-11.md`, `gpu_roi_paste/README.md`, `color_ema/README.md`, `swap_batch_render_2026-10-11.md`) — baseline-only knowledge, useful as negative results.
- **Phase 7/8 real-video validation OPEN**, `.fsz` archives lacked pitch/inversion plates (`PHASE6_POSE_QUALITY.md`).
- **Live-cam 45 ms target unverified** (`LIVE_MODE.md`).
- **Test-suite shape**: 243 of 717 files are harness/probe/diag scripts (not collected as pytest tests); `test_live_acceptance_suite.py` has no docstring and drives a live server over HTTP (needs running backend) — [INFERENCE] not run in CI. Tests by regex: 6453 `def test_`.
- **UI**: `store/recipeStore.ts` has no consumer (`historyStore.js` header says so) — dead typed store; `src/App.css` empty header; `react-ui/package-lock.json` 72 KB.
